\displaystyle|Z^{k+2;l+1,m+1}\varphi_{\gamma}(s,u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{-l-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2},

(6.220)

\displaystyle|Z^{k+1;l,m+1}\varphi_{\gamma}(s,u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{1-l-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2},

(6.221)

\displaystyle|Z^{k+1;l,m+1}\phi(s,u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{2-l-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}.

(6.222)

In the end of this section, it should be emphasized that under the assumptions
(\star)
,
one has obtained the estimates in Lemma
6.1
, Proposition
6.1
-
6.3
with
the corresponding bounds dependent on
M
.
Furthermore, improved results are obtained in Corollary
6.1
-
6.3
with the bounds independent of
M
.
Therefore, if starting with Corollary
6.1
-
6.3
and repeating
the related procedure as in Section
6
, we can improve the conclusions in Lemma
6.1
and
Propositions
6.1
-
6.3

such that all the related constants are independent of
M
when
k\leq N-3
.
From now on, when these propositions are applied, the special constant
M
will be removed in the related
L^{\infty}

norms since
N
could be chosen large enough.

7.
Energy estimates for the linearized equation

It remains to close the bootstrap assumption
(\star)
for the
N-th
and
(N+1)-th
order derivatives, for which the approach in Section
6
fails to apply.
To this end, we will construct some suitable energies for the higher order derivatives of
\varphi_{\gamma}

to improve the related
L^{\infty}
norms. Note that
\varphi_{\gamma}
and
\phi
satisfy the nonlinear equations (
4.108
) and (
4.116
) respectively, and each
derivative of
\varphi_{\gamma}
or
\phi
also fulfills analogous equations with the same metric. Therefore, in this section, we focus on
the energy estimates for the smooth
solution
\Psi
to the following linear equation

\mu\Box_{g}\Psi=\Phi,

(7.223)

where
\Psi
and its derivatives vanish on
C_{0}^{s}
.
We will choose a suitable multiplier
V\Psi

(
V
is some vector field) and integrate
\mu(\Box_{g}\Psi)(V\Psi)
in domain
D^{s,u}

such that some appropriate energies can be found. To this end, set the
energy-momentum tensor field
of
\Psi
in (
7.223
) as

Q_{\alpha\beta}=Q_{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]:=(\partial_{\alpha}\Psi)(\partial_{\beta}\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}g_{\alpha\beta}g^{\nu\lambda}(\partial_{\nu}\Psi)(\partial_{\lambda}\Psi).

Then (
4.106
) yields

Q_{\alpha\beta}=(\partial_{\alpha}\Psi)(\partial_{\beta}\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}g_{\alpha\beta}\big\{|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}-\mu^{-1}(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)(\mathring{L}\Psi)\big\}
. Thus, the components of
Q_{\alpha\beta}
relative to
\{\mathring{L},\mathring{\underline{L}},X_{1},X_{2},X_{3}\}
are

\begin{split}&Q_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}=(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2},\quad Q_{\mathring{\underline{L}}\mathring{\underline{L}}}=(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)^{2},\quad Q_{\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}}=\mu|\not{d}\Psi|^{2},\\
&Q_{\mathring{L}A}=(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\not{d}_{A}\Psi),\quad Q_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}=(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)(\not{d}_{A}\Psi),\\
&Q_{AB}=(\not{d}_{A}\Psi)(\not{d}_{B}\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}\not{g}_{AB}\{|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}-\mu^{-1}(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)(\mathring{L}\Psi)\big\}.\end{split}

Let
V_{1}:=\varrho^{2\epsilon}\mathring{L}
and
V_{2}:=\mathring{\underline{L}}
with
0<\epsilon<\frac{1}{2}

being any fixed constant. The energies
E_{i}[\Psi](s,u)
and fluxes
F_{i}[\Psi](s,u)

(i=1,2)

are defined as

\displaystyle E_{1}[\Psi](s,u):=\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}\mu\varrho^{2\epsilon}\{(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\},

(7.224)

\displaystyle E_{2}[\Psi](s,u):=\frac{1}{2}\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}\{(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)^{2}+\mu^{2}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\},

(7.225)

\displaystyle F_{1}[\Psi](s,u):=\displaystyle\int_{C_{u}^{s}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2},

(7.226)

\displaystyle F_{2}[\Psi](s,u):=\displaystyle\int_{C_{u}^{s}}\mu|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}.

(7.227)

It follows from integration by parts in
D^{s,u}
for
(\mu\Box_{g}\Psi)(V_{i}\Psi)
that

\begin{split}&E_{i}[\Psi](s,u)-E_{i}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+F_{i}[\Psi](s,u)\\
&=-\displaystyle\int_{D^{s,u}}\Phi\cdot V_{i}\Psi-\int_{D^{s,u}}\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{i})}\pi_{\alpha\beta},\qquad i=1,2,\end{split}

(7.228)

where
{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(V_{i})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}
is the deformation tensor with respect to the vector field
V_{i}
.

To compute
Q^{\alpha\beta}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(V_{i})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}
in (
7.228
), one can use the
components of the metric in the frame
\{\mathring{L},\underline{\mathring{L}},X_{1},X_{2},X_{3}\}
, given in (
4.106
), to derive directly that

\begin{split}&-\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}\\
=&-\frac{1}{4}\mu^{-1}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}}Q_{\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}}-\frac{1}{8}\mu^{-1}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\mathring{\underline{L}}\mathring{\underline{L}}}Q_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}+\frac{1}{2}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}^{A}Q_{\mathring{L}A}-\frac{1}{2}\mu{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(V_{1})}\not{\pi}^{AB}Q_{AB}\\
=&(\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2\epsilon}\mathring{L}\mu+\mu(\epsilon+\frac{1}{2})\varrho^{2\epsilon-1})|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\big(\epsilon(\mu-2)\varrho^{2\epsilon-1}-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2\epsilon}\mathring{L}\mu\big)(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}\\
&+\varrho^{2\epsilon}(\not{d}^{A}\mu+2\mu\zeta^{A})(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\not{d}_{A}\Psi)-\mu\varrho^{2\epsilon}\check{\chi}_{AB}(\not{d}^{A}\Psi)(\not{d}^{B}\Psi)+\frac{1}{2}\mu\varrho^{2\epsilon}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2\epsilon}\textrm{tr}\chi(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)\end{split}

(7.229)

and

\begin{split}&-\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{2})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}\\
=&\frac{1}{2}(\mathring{\underline{L}}\mu+\mu\mathring{L}\mu)|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}-(2\mu\zeta^{A}+\not{d}^{A}\mu)(\not{d}_{A}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)-\mu\not{d}^{A}\mu(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\not{d}_{A}\Psi)\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\big(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}+\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\big)(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)-\mu\big(\mu{\chi}^{AB}+{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}^{AB}\big)(\not{d}_{A}\Psi)(\not{d}_{B}\Psi)\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\mu\big(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}+\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\big)|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}.\end{split}

(7.230)

Next, applying the results in Sect.
6
to estimate all the coefficients in (
7.229
) and (
7.230
), one has

\begin{split}&\int_{D^{s,u}}-\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{u}\int_{C_{u^{\prime}}^{s}}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\int_{C_{u^{\prime}}^{s}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}|\mathring{L}\Psi|^{2}+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{2\epsilon-2}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}^{u}}|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|^{2}\\
\overset{0<\epsilon<\frac{1}{2}}{\lesssim}&\int_{0}^{u}F_{2}[\Psi](s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1}[\Psi](s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{2\epsilon-2}E_{2}[\Psi](\tau,u)d\tau\end{split}

(7.231)

and

\begin{split}&\int_{D^{s,u}}-\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{2})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}\int_{C_{u^{\prime}}^{s}}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}\int_{C_{u^{\prime}}^{s}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}|\mathring{L}\Psi|^{2}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}^{u}}|\underline{\mathring{L}}\Psi|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{2}[\Psi](s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1}[\Psi](s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}E_{2}[\Psi](\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(7.232)

Substituting (
7.231
) and (
7.232
) into (
7.228
) and using the Gronwall’s inequality yield

\begin{split}&E_{1}[\Psi](s,u)+F_{1}[\Psi](s,u)\\
\lesssim&E_{1}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+\displaystyle\int_{D^{s,u}}|\varrho^{2\epsilon}\Phi(\mathring{L}\Psi)|+\int_{0}^{u}F_{2}[\Psi](s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{2\epsilon-2}E_{2}[\Psi](\tau,u)d\tau\end{split}

(7.233)

and

\begin{split}&E_{2}[\Psi](s,u)+F_{2}[\Psi](s,u)\\
\lesssim&E_{2}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+\displaystyle\int_{D^{s,u}}|\Phi(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)|+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1}[\Psi](s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(7.234)

Taking the linear summation of (
7.233
) and (
7.234
), and then utilizing Gronwall’s inequality again, one
has that for
\epsilon\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
,

\begin{split}&\delta E_{2}[\Psi](s,u)+\delta F_{2}[\Psi](s,u)+E_{1}[\Psi](s,u)+F_{1}[\Psi](s,u)\\
\lesssim&\delta E_{2}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+E_{1}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+\delta\int_{D^{s,u}}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|+\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{L}\Psi|.\end{split}

(7.235)

To obtain the energy estimate for
\varphi_{\gamma}
and its derivatives, we will apply (
7.235
) to
\Psi=\Psi_{\gamma}^{k+1}=Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}
and

\Phi=\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}=\mu\Box_{g}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k+1}
(
k\leq 2N-6
). Note that

\begin{split}\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}&=\mu[\Box_{g},Z]\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+Z\big(\mu\Box_{g}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\big)-(Z\mu)\Box_{g}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\\
&=\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}({{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\alpha})+(Z+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z)}\lambda)\Phi_{\gamma}^{k},\end{split}

(7.236)

where

\begin{split}&{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\alpha}=\big({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\nu\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}g_{\nu\alpha}(g_{\kappa\lambda}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi^{\kappa\lambda})\big)g^{\nu\beta}\partial_{\beta}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k},\\
&{\vphantom{\lambda}}{}^{(Z)}\lambda=\frac{1}{2}g_{\kappa\nu}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi^{\kappa\nu}-\mu^{-1}Z\mu,\\
&\Psi_{\gamma}^{0}=\varphi_{\gamma},\quad\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}=\mu\Box_{g}\varphi_{\gamma},\end{split}

(7.237)

and
\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}
is given by
the right hand side of (
4.108
).
Consequently, for
\Psi_{\gamma}^{k+1}=Z_{k+1}Z_{k}\cdots Z_{1}\varphi_{\gamma}
with
Z_{m}\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R_{ij}\}
,
then it follows from (
7.236
) and an induction argument that

\begin{split}\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}=&\sum_{m=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-m}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+2-m})}\lambda\big)\big(\mu\mathscr{D}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}C_{\gamma}^{k-m}}_{,\alpha}\big)\\
&+\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z_{k+1})}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\alpha}+\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{1})}\lambda\big)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0},\qquad k\geq 1,\\
\Phi_{\gamma}^{1}=&\big(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{1})}\lambda\big)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}+\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z_{1})}C_{\gamma}^{0}}_{,\alpha}.\end{split}

(7.238)

In order to estimate
{\vphantom{\lambda}}{}^{(Z)}\lambda
and
\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma,\alpha}^{k}
more conveniently, one can rewrite

g_{\kappa\nu}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi^{\kappa\nu}=-2\mu^{-1}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}.

By (
4.89
)-(
4.91
), one has

\begin{split}&{\vphantom{\lambda}}{}^{(T)}\lambda=\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi},\qquad{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}\lambda=\varrho\textrm{tr}_{\not{g}}\check{\chi}+4,\qquad{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(R_{ij})}\lambda=\frac{1}{2}\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}.\end{split}

(7.239)

In addition, in the null frame
\{\mathring{\underline{L}},\mathring{L},X_{1},X_{2},X_{3}\}
, the
term
\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\alpha}
can be written as

\begin{split}\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\alpha}=&-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\big({{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{\underline{L}}}\big)-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{\underline{L}}\big({{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{L}}\big)+\not{\nabla}^{A}\big(\mu{{\vphantom{\not{C}}}{}^{(Z)}\not{C}_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,A}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\big(\mathring{L}\mu+\mu\textrm{tr}{\chi}+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\big){{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{L}}-\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{\underline{L}}},\end{split}

(7.240)

where

\begin{split}&{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{L}}={\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}(\not{d}^{A}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})-\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k},\\
&{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{\underline{L}}}=-2({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{LT}+\mu^{-1}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(Z)}\pi_{TT})(\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})+{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}(\not{d}^{A}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})-\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k},\\
&\mu{{\vphantom{\not{C}}}{}^{(Z)}\not{C}_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,A}=-\frac{1}{2}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}(\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})-\frac{1}{2}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})+{\vphantom{{\pi}}}^{(Z)}{\pi}_{\mathring{L}T}(\not{d}_{A}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})\\
&\qquad\qquad\quad+\mu({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}\not{g}_{AB})(\not{d}^{B}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}).\end{split}

(7.241)

A direct substitution of (
7.241
) into (
7.240
) would lead to a lengthy and complicated equation for
\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\alpha}

which seems to be difficult to analyze. To overcome this difficulty,
one can decompose
\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\alpha}
into the following three parts as in
[
36
]
:

\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\alpha}={\vphantom{D}}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{k}+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k}+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{k},

(7.242)

where

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{k}=&\mu({\vphantom{{\not{\pi}}}}^{(Z)}{\not{\pi}}^{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{{\not{\pi}}}}^{(Z)}{\not{\pi}}\not{g}^{AB})\not{\nabla}^{2}_{AB}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}(\not{d}^{A}\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}(\not{d}^{A}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})\\
&+({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}+{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\tilde{T}T})(\mathring{L}^{2}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})+{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}\not{\triangle}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}\big(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\big),\end{split}

(7.243)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k}=&\mathring{L}({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}+{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\tilde{T}T})\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}-\big(\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}^{A}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}-\frac{1}{4}\mathring{\underline{L}}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})\big)\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\\
&-(\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}-\not{d}_{A}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T})\not{d}^{A}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\not{\nabla}^{B}\big(\mu{\vphantom{{\not{\pi}}}}^{(Z)}{\not{\pi}}_{AB}-\frac{\mu}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}\not{g}_{AB}\big)\not{d}^{A}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}(\not{\nabla}^{A}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A})\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}-\frac{1}{2}(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A})\not{d}^{A}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\frac{1}{4}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k},\end{split}

(7.244)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{k}=&\Big\{\textrm{tr}{\chi}({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}+{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\tilde{T}T})+\frac{1}{4}(\mu\textrm{tr}{\chi}+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}+\frac{1}{2}\not{d}^{A}\mu{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}\Big\}\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\Big\{(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})({\eta}_{A}+\mu\zeta_{A})-(\mathring{L}\mu){\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}-\textrm{tr}{\chi}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}+2{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}^{B}\chi_{AB}\\
&\qquad\quad+2{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{B}(\mu\chi_{AB}+{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{AB})-(\mu\textrm{tr}\chi+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}){\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}\Big\}\not{d}^{A}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}.\end{split}

(7.245)

Note that all the terms in
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{k}
are the products of the deformation tensor and the
second order derivatives of
\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}
except the last term. The reason putting
\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}
together in the last term of (
7.243
)
is that since
\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}
is the
derivative of
\varphi_{\gamma}
, then by equation (
5.128
),
\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}=H_{\gamma}+\mu\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}

admits the better smallness and faster time-decay rate
than those of
\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
and
\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
separately.
In addition,
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k}
consists of all the products of the first order derivatives of the deformation
tensor and the first order derivatives of
\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}
, and
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{k}
consists of the other remaining terms.

With the explicit expressions (
7.238
)-(
7.239
) and (
7.242
)-(
7.245
) at hand, one can now estimate the last two integrals of (
7.235
).
To this end, one can define the corresponding weighted energy and flux as in
[
36
]
:

\displaystyle E_{i,m+1}(s,u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\gamma=0}^{4}\sum_{Z\in\{\mathring{L},T,R_{ab}\}}\delta^{2l}E_{i}[Z^{m}\varphi_{\gamma}](s,u),\quad i=1,2,

(7.246)

\displaystyle F_{i,m+1}(s,u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\gamma=0}^{4}\sum_{Z\in\{\mathring{L},T,R_{ab}\}}\delta^{2l}F_{i}[Z^{m}\varphi_{\gamma}](s,u),\quad i=1,2,

(7.247)

\displaystyle E_{i,\leq m+1}(s,u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{0\leq n\leq m}E_{i,n+1}(s,u),\quad i=1,2,

(7.248)

\displaystyle F_{i,\leq m+1}(s,u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{0\leq n\leq m}F_{i,n+1}(s,u),\quad i=1,2,

(7.249)

where
l
is the number of
T
in
Z^{m}
. These weighted energies will be estimated in the subsequent sections.

8.
Higher order
L^{2}
estimates

In this section, we shall establish the higher order
L^{2}
estimates for some related quantities
so that the last two terms on the right hand side of (
7.235
) can be absorbed by the left hand side, and hence the higher order energy estimates on (
4.108
) can
be derived.

Note that the energies and fluxes in Section
7
are all defined for
\varphi_{\gamma}
(see (
7.246
)-(
7.249
)).
In order to use these energies to control
L^{2}
norms of
\phi
and its derivatives, one requires the following results,
whose analogous statements
can be found in Lemma 7.3 of
[
36
]
for 3-D case.

Lemma 8.1
.

For any function
\psi\in C^{1}(D^{s,u})
which vanishes on
C_{0}
, it holds that for small
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\int_{S_{s,u}}\psi^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}|T\psi|^{2}\lesssim\delta\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}\big(|\mathring{\underline{L}}\psi|^{2}+\mu^{2}|\mathring{L}\psi|^{2}\big),

(8.250)

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}\psi^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{2}\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}|T\psi|^{2}\lesssim\delta^{2}\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}\big(|\mathring{\underline{L}}\psi|^{2}+\mu^{2}|\mathring{L}\psi|^{2}\big).

(8.251)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\int_{S_{s,u}}\psi^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\big(E_{2}[\psi](s,u)+\varrho^{-2\epsilon}E_{1}[\psi](s,u)\big),

(8.252)

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}\psi^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{2}\big(E_{2}[\psi](s,u)+\varrho^{-2\epsilon}E_{1}[\psi](s,u)\big).

(8.253)

Proof.

For any function
f\in C^{1}(D^{s,u})
vanishing on
C_{0}
, by (
4.89
) and (
4.98
), one has

\begin{split}\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\int_{S_{s,u}}fd\nu_{\not{g}}&=\int_{S_{s,u}}\big(Tf+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\cdot f\big)d\nu_{\not{g}}.\end{split}

This leads to

|\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\int_{S_{s,u}}fd\nu_{\not{g}}|\lesssim\int_{S_{s,u}}(|Tf|+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}|f|)

(8.254)

by (
6.197
).
Setting
f=\psi^{2}
in (
8.254
) and then integrating from
0
to
u
yield

\begin{split}\int_{S_{s,u}}\psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{s}^{u}}(|T\psi|\cdot|\psi|+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\psi^{2})\lesssim\int_{0}^{u}\int_{S_{s,u^{\prime}}}\delta^{-1}\psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}du^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{u}\int_{S_{s,u^{\prime}}}\delta|T\psi|^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}du^{\prime}.\end{split}

Thus it follows from Gronwall’s inequality that

\displaystyle\int_{S_{s,u}}\psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}\lesssim\int_{0}^{u}\int_{S_{s,u^{\prime}}}\delta|T\psi|^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}du^{\prime}.

(8.255)

Taking
T=\frac{1}{2}\mathring{\underline{L}}-\frac{1}{2}\mu\mathring{L}
in (
8.255
) leads to (
8.250
), and hence (
8.251
) holds.
∎

For any integer
k
, with the help of (
8.251
), one has

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1+l}\|[T,Z^{k+1}]\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1+l}\|Z^{k+1}(\mu\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})},\end{split}

(8.256)

where
l
and
l_{m}
(
m=0,1
) are the numbers of
T
in
Z^{k+1}
and
Z^{k_{m}}
respectively, and
1\leq k_{m}\leq k+1-m
.
Utilizing (
8.253
) to estimate the last term of (
8.256
), one then gets from (
8.256
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2}s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.257)

Based on (
8.257
), due to
T\phi=-\mu(g^{0i}+\check{L}^{i}+\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho})\varphi_{i}
and
\mathring{L}\phi=\varphi_{0}+\check{L}^{i}\varphi_{i}+\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}\varphi_{i}
, it holds that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\big(\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|Z^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\big(\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|Z^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)+\delta^{4-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-4}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-4}\delta^{l_{1}}\big(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}\end{split}

(8.258)

and

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\big(\|Z^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.259)

Similarly as for (
8.257
) and (
8.258
), one derives

\begin{split}&\|R^{k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2}s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}\end{split}

(8.260)

and

\begin{split}\|R^{k+1}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\big(\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|R^{\leq k+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\not{d}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-4}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{5-4\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},\end{split}

(8.261)

where
\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}=\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{k+1}\|R^{i}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}
.
In addition,

\begin{split}\|R^{k}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\leq&\delta\|[T,R^{k}]\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{k}[T,\mathring{L}]\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{k}\mathring{L}(\mu\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k-1}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{\leq k}(\mathring{L}\mu)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{\leq k}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{\leq k}(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}.\end{split}

Note that
\mathring{L}\mu
and
\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i}
satisfy (
4.86
) and (
4.92
) respectively. Then combining (
8.260
) and (
8.261
) yields

\begin{split}\|R^{k}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2}s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}t^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.262)

Note that to estimate
\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

in Proposition
8.1
below, one needs to deal with
\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}
first, for which the following elementary estimate will be used.

Lemma 8.2
.

For any function
f\in C(D^{s,u})
, set

F(s,u,\vartheta):=\int_{t_{0}}^{s}f(\tau,u,\vartheta)d\tau.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with suitably small
\delta>0
, it holds that

\|F\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\varrho(s,u)^{3/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\frac{1}{\varrho(\tau,u)^{3/2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau.

(8.263)

Proof.

The proof is exactly similar to that of Lemma 12.57 in
[
39
]
and thus omitted here.
∎

Based on the preparations above, we are ready to derive the
L^{2}
estimates for the quantities including

{\check{\chi}}
,
{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}
,
{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}
,
\check{L^{i}}
,
\upsilon_{ij}
,
x^{j}
and so on.

Proposition 8.1
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with suitably small
\delta>0
, it holds that for
{k\leq 2N-6}
,

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}+s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\check{L}^{j}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\check{\varrho}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k+1}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}s^{3/2}+s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|{Z}^{k+2}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}s^{5/2}+s^{2-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k+1}R\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}s^{5/2}+s^{2-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\upsilon_{ij}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}+s^{2-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

where
\tilde{E}_{i,\leq k+2}(s,u)=\sup_{t_{0}\leq\tau\leq s}E_{i,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)

(i=1,2)
.

Proof.

It follows from Lemma
4.4
and (
4.99
) that explicit expressions of
\varrho\mathring{L}\check{L}^{i}
,
T\check{L}^{i}
and
R_{jl}\check{L}^{i}

can be given since
\varrho\mathring{L}\check{L}^{i}=\varrho\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i}-\check{L}^{i}
,
T\check{L}^{i}=T\mathring{L}^{i}+\frac{\mu}{\varrho}g^{0i}+\frac{\mu}{\varrho}\check{L}^{i}+\frac{\mu-1}{\varrho^{2}}x^{i}
and
R_{jl}\check{L}^{i}={R_{jl}^{A}}\not{d}_{A}\check{L}^{i}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k}(\varrho\mathring{L}\check{L}^{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{{0}}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{{0}}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{{0}}}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})},

\displaystyle\delta^{l+1}\|Z^{k}(T\check{L}^{i})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{{0}}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{{0}}}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{{0}}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k}R\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}R\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})},

where

is the number of

in

,

).

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}R\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\

&+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.264)

Note that

holds by Lemma

.
This and

yield

. Thus,

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}R\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}\\

(8.265)

Analogously, due to

and

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\check{\varrho}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.287)

As in (
8.260
), all the vector fields

Z
in (
8.275
)-(
8.284
) can be replaced by the rotation vector fields
R_{ij}
to obtain the following analogous estimates

\begin{split}\|R^{k+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},\\
\end{split}

\displaystyle\|R^{k+2}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{1/2}s^{5/2}+s^{2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k+1}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{1/2}s^{3/2}+s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\|R^{k+1}\upsilon_{ij}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}+s^{2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k+1}R\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{1/2}s^{5/2}+s^{2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

(8.288)

\displaystyle+s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

\displaystyle+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.

This together with (
8.260
)-(
8.262
) yields

\begin{split}&\|R^{k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{k+1}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2}s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},\end{split}

(8.289)

\begin{split}&\|R^{k}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2}s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}s^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},\end{split}

(8.290)

\begin{split}\|R^{k+1}\check{\varrho}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-1}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.291)

Note that all the terms in the left hand side of (
8.275
)-(
8.284
) are controlled by the
L^{2}-
norms of
the derivatives of
\check{\chi}
and
\mu
, which will be treated as follows.

•

When
Z\in\{R_{ij}:1\leq i<j\leq 4\}
, as in the proof of Lemma
6.1
, one needs to deal with each term in

\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\check{\chi}
(see (
6.188
) and (
5.165
)).
Due to
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R_{ij}}\not{d}x^{a}=\not{d}\Omega_{ij}^{a}+\not{d}\big(\upsilon_{ij}(g^{0a}+\check{L}^{a}+\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})\big)
, then

|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\not{d}x^{a}|\lesssim 1+t^{-1}|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{ij}|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1}\big(|R^{\leq k}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{a}|\big).

(8.292)

This implies

\begin{split}&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}(F_{B\mathring{L}}\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\phi)|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}(F_{A\mathring{L}}\not{d}_{B}\mathring{L}\phi)|\\
\lesssim&\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=k}|R^{k_{1}}(F_{i\beta}\mathring{L}^{\beta})|\cdot|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k_{2}}\not{d}x^{i}|\cdot|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k_{3}}\not{d}\mathring{L}\phi|\\
\lesssim&\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k}\big(|R^{k_{1}}\phi|+|R^{k_{1}}\varphi|+|R^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k_{2}}\not{d}x^{i}|\big)|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k_{3}}\not{d}\mathring{L}\phi|\\
\lesssim&|\not{d}R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\phi|+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\big(s^{-1}|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{ab}|+|R^{\leq k}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}|\big).\end{split}

(8.293)

Similarly, one has

\begin{split}&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}(G_{B\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}(G_{A\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}_{B}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\big(F_{AB}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi\big)|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\big(G_{AB}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)|\\
\lesssim&|\not{d}R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\big(s^{-1}|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{ab}|+|R^{\leq k}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}|\big)\end{split}

(8.294)

and

\begin{split}&|R^{k}(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\mathring{L}\phi)|+|R^{k}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})|+|R^{k}(F_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}\mathring{L}\phi)|\\
&+|R^{k}(G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}(F_{B\mathring{L}}\not{d}_{A}\phi)|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}(G_{B\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}_{A}\varphi_{\gamma})|\\
\lesssim&|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi|+|\not{d}R^{\leq k}\phi|+|\not{d}R^{\leq k}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\big(s^{-1}|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{ab}|+|R^{\leq k}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}|\big).\end{split}

(8.295)

In addition, for the term
F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\phi
, one can apply the part (a) of Lemma
4.8
and Corollary
5.1
to get

\begin{split}&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\big(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\phi\big)|\\
\lesssim&\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k}|R^{k_{1}}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}|\cdot|[\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k_{2}},\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}]\phi+\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}R^{k_{2}}\phi|\\
\lesssim&s^{-2}|R^{\leq k+2}\phi|+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}|+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{ab}|\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\big(|R^{\leq k}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}|\big)\end{split}

(8.296)

and

\begin{split}&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\big(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)|\\
\lesssim&s^{-1}|\not{d}R^{\leq k+1}\varphi|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{ab}|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\big(|R^{\leq k}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}|\big).\end{split}

(8.297)

Substituting (
8.293
)-(
8.297
) into (
5.165
) yields

\begin{split}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\check{\chi}|\lesssim&s^{-1}|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\phi|+s^{-1}|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi|+s^{-2}|R^{\leq k+2}\phi|+s^{-1}|\not{d}R^{\leq k+1}\varphi|\\
&+|\not{d}R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\phi|+|\not{d}R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\big(s^{-1}|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{ab}|+|R^{\leq k}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}|\big)\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}|+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\not{d}x^{i}|.\end{split}

(8.298)

Next we come to estimate the
L^{2}
norms of
R^{k+2}\phi
,
\not{d}R^{k}\mathring{L}\phi
and
R^{k}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi
.

(1)

As
R_{ij}=\Omega_{ij}-\upsilon_{ij}\tilde{T}
, then (
8.292
), together
with (
8.288
) and (
8.289
), implies that

\begin{split}&\|R^{k+1}R_{ij}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}=\|R^{k+1}\big((\Omega_{ij}^{l}-\upsilon_{ij}\tilde{T}^{l})\varphi_{l}\big)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k}\|\big(s|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k_{1}}\not{d}x|+|R^{k_{1}+1}(\upsilon_{ij}\tilde{T}^{l})|\big)R^{k_{2}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\|R^{k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&s\|R^{k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\upsilon_{ij}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}x^{a}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\big(\|R^{\leq k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|R^{\leq k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|R^{\leq k+1}\check{L}^{l}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.299)

(2)

Note that

\not{d}R^{k}\mathring{L}\phi=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\not{d}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\big((\not{d}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}+(\not{d}x^{i})\mathring{L}\varphi_{i}\big).

(8.300)

One gets from (
4.94
) that

(\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}=\chi_{AB}{\not{d}}^{B}\phi-\big\{(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\tilde{T}}(X_{A})+\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\tilde{T}}(X_{A})\big\}\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{i}+\Lambda_{AB}\not{d}^{B}\phi.

It follows from (
8.288
)-(
8.290
) that

\begin{split}&\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\big((\not{d}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}\big)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-4}\big(\|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\not{d}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)+s^{-2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\big(\|R^{\leq k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k-1}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.301)

In addition, by (
8.292
),

\begin{split}&\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\big((\not{d}x^{i})\mathring{L}\varphi_{i}\big)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{jl}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\big(\|R^{\leq k}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|R^{\leq k}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.302)

Combining (
8.301
)-(
8.302
) and (
8.300
) yields

\begin{split}\|\not{d}R^{k}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.303)

(3)

Note that

\begin{split}&|R^{k}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi|=|R^{k}\big((\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}+\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}\big)|\\
\lesssim&\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k}\Big(|R^{k_{1}}(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i})R^{k_{2}}\varphi_{i}|+(|R^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}|+s^{-1}|R^{k_{1}}x^{i}|)|R^{k_{2}}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i}|\Big).\end{split}

Since
\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i}
satisfies (
4.92
), then

\begin{split}|R^{k_{1}}(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i})|\lesssim&|R^{\leq k_{1}}\mathring{L}\phi|+|R^{\leq k_{1}}\mathring{L}\varphi|+s^{-1}|R^{\leq k_{1}+1}\phi|+s^{-1}|R^{\leq k_{1}+1}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}(|R^{\leq k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}|+s^{-1}|R^{\leq k_{1}+1}x^{i}|)\end{split}

and it follows from (
8.292
) that

\begin{split}s^{-1}|R^{k_{1}}x^{i}|\cdot|R^{k_{2}}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i}|\lesssim&|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{jl}|\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}(|R^{\leq k}\phi|+|R^{\leq k}\varphi|+|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}|).\end{split}

Thus,

\begin{split}&\|R^{k}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k}\upsilon_{jl}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}|R^{\leq k}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5}\|R^{\leq k+1}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.304)

Substituting (
8.299
), (
8.303
) and (
8.304
) into (
8.298
), and using (
8.288
) and (
6.188
), we have

\begin{split}&\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+t^{-1}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-4}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k-1}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.305)

Next we estimate the
L^{2}-
norm of
\mathring{L}R^{k+1}\mu
. For any
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R_{ij}\}
, by virtue
of (
6.195
), (
4.86
) and (
5.124
), one can get

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\big(\delta^{3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{0}}\not{d}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{\varepsilon_{0}}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\bar{Z}^{k_{1}}\upsilon_{ij}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\big(\|\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\bar{Z}^{l_{0}}\check{\varrho}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\big(s\|T\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{l_{0}}\big(\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\big(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big),\end{split}

(8.306)

where
l
is the number of
T
in
\bar{Z}^{k+1}
.
If all
\bar{Z}
in (
8.306
) are chosen from
\{R_{ij}\}
, then it follows from (
8.288
)-(
8.291
) and (
8.303
) that

\begin{split}\|\mathring{L}R^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\|R^{\leq k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.307)

Let
F(s,u,\vartheta)=\varrho(s,u)^{2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}(s,u,\vartheta)|-\varrho(t_{0},u)^{2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}(t_{0},u,\vartheta)|
in (
8.263
). Since for any 2-form
\xi
on
S_{s,u}
,
|\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2}|\xi|)|\lesssim\varrho^{2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\xi|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}(\varrho^{2}|\xi|)
holds, then

\begin{split}\|\varrho^{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{3/2}+\varrho^{3/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\Big(&\|\varrho^{2}(\tau,u)\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-2}\|\varrho^{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\Big)d\tau.\end{split}

(8.308)

Applying (
8.305
) and Grownwall’s equality for (
8.308
) yields

\begin{split}s^{1/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1/2-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1/2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau.\end{split}

(8.309)

Analogously, (
8.307
) gives that

\begin{split}s^{-3/2}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu&\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau.\end{split}

(8.310)

Let

\begin{split}G_{1}(s,u)=&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1/2-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1/2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau\end{split}

and

\begin{split}G_{2}(s,u)=&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{\leq k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}d\tau.\end{split}

Then

\displaystyle\partial_{s}G_{1}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}G_{2}+s^{-1/2-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta s^{-1/2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}},

\displaystyle\partial_{s}G_{2}\lesssim\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}G_{1}+s^{-3/2-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}}.

This, together with
s^{1/2}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim G_{1}

and
s^{-3/2}\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim G_{2}
by (
8.309
) and (
8.310
), yields

\partial_{s}(s^{-1/4}G_{1}+\delta G_{2})\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/4}(s^{-1/4}G_{1}+\delta G_{2})+s^{-3/4-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta s^{-3/4}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}},

which gives

s^{-1/4}G_{1}+\delta G_{2}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+s^{1/4}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta s^{1/4}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim s^{-1/2}G_{1}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/4}+\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}},

(8.311)

\displaystyle\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim s^{3/2}G_{2}\lesssim\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}+\delta^{-1}s^{7/4}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+s^{7/4}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}}.

(8.312)

Substituting (
8.312
) and (
8.311
) into (
8.309
) and (
8.310
), respectively, one has

\displaystyle\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}+s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

(8.313)

\displaystyle\|R^{[1,k+1]}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.

(8.314)

•

If
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{T}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1
, then
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}[\not{\mathcal{L}}_{T},\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{p_{2}}]\check{\chi}+\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{p_{2}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{T}\check{\chi}
, and hence
it follows from Lemma
4.8
, (
5.166
) and (
8.275
)-(
8.285
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\big(\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\big(\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta t^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.315)

If
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\varrho\mathring{L}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1
, then
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}[\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\varrho\mathring{L}},\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{p_{2}}]\check{\chi}+\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}^{p_{2}}(\varrho\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\check{\chi})
, and it follows from (
5.165
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\big(\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\big(s\delta^{l_{2}}\|Z^{k_{2}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.316)

Therefore, for any
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R_{ij}\}
, by (
8.313
), (
8.315
) and (
8.316
), we obtain

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}+\delta s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.317)

•

If
Z^{k+1}\mu=\bar{Z}^{k+1}\mu
with
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R_{ij}\}
,
then substituting the corresponding estimates in (
8.275
)-(
8.287
) into (
8.306
) and applying (
8.317
) show that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{E_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

Taking
F(s,u,\vartheta)=\delta^{l}\bar{Z}^{k+1}\mu(s,u,\vartheta)-\delta^{l}\bar{Z}^{k+1}\mu(t_{0},u,\vartheta)
in (
8.263
) yields

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.318)

If
Z^{k+1}\mu=Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\mu
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=k
, it follows from (
4.86
), (
5.124
), (
8.275
)-(
8.285
) and (
8.317
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}=\delta^{l}\|Z^{p_{1}}[\varrho\mathring{L},\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}]\mu+Z^{p_{1}}\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{2}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.319)

Thus, for any
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R_{ij}\}
, (
8.318
) and (
8.319
) yield

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.320)

Applying Gronwall’s inequality for
s^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}
, one can get

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.321)

This, together with (
8.317
), gives

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1/2}+s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(8.322)

Substituting (
8.321
)-(
8.322
) into (
8.275
)-(
8.284
) and (
8.287
) eventually
completes the proof of Proposition 9.1.
∎

On the other hand, the proof of Proposition 9.1 also gives the following energy estimates for
\phi
.

Corollary 8.1
.

Under the assumption
(\star)
with suitably small
\delta>0
, it holds that for
k\leq 2N-6
,

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}R\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.

Proof.

The estimates for
Z^{k+1}\phi
,
Z^{k+1}T\phi
and
Z^{k+1}\mathring{L}\phi
can be directly
got by (
8.285
), (
8.320
), (
8.321
) and (
8.322
). In addition, due to
R\phi=Rx^{i}\cdot\varphi_{i}
, then

\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}R\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}Rx^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})},

which gives the
L^{2}
control of
Z^{k+1}R\phi
with the help of Proposition
8.1
.
∎

9.
L^{2}
estimates for the highest order derivatives of
\not{\nabla}\chi
and
\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu

It is clear from the energy estimate (
7.235
) and the expression (
7.236
) that the highest order derivatives of
\varphi

in the energy estimate for (
7.236
) are of
2N-4
. On the other hand, by the expression of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k}

in (
7.244
), the highest order derivatives of
\chi
and
\mu
in (
7.235
) are of
2N-5
and
2N-4
respectively. However,
it follows from Proposition
8.1
that the
L^{2}
estimates on the
2N-5
order derivatives of
{\chi}

and the
2N-4
order derivatives of

\mu
should be controlled by the energies of
\varphi
with the orders
2N-3
,
which goes beyond the range of energies containing up to
2N-4
order derivatives of
\varphi
.
To overcome this difficulty, as in
[
9
]
and
[
39
]
, one needs to
treat
\textrm{tr}{\chi}
and
\not{\triangle}\mu
with the corresponding highest order derivatives.

Note that once the estimate of
\not{\triangle}\mu
is established, one can use the elliptic estimate to treat
\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu
.
We start with the estimate on the Riemann curvature
\not{\mathcal{R}}
of
\not{g}
.

Lemma 9.1
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with suitably small
\delta>0
, the Riemann curvature
\not{\mathcal{R}}
of
\not{g}
in
D^{s,u}

satisfies

|\not{\mathcal{R}}|\lesssim s^{-2}.

(9.323)

Proof.

Let
\not{\Gamma}_{AB}^{C}
be the Christoffel symbols of
\not{g}
, that is,

\not{\Gamma}_{AB}^{C}=\frac{1}{2}\not{g}^{CD}\big\{X_{A}(\not{g}_{DB})+X_{B}(\not{g}_{AD})-X_{D}(\not{g}_{AB})\big\}.

Take the derivative of
\not{\Gamma}_{AB}^{C}
with respect to
s
to get

\mathring{L}\not{\Gamma}_{AB}^{C}=\not{g}^{CD}(\not{\nabla}_{A}\check{\chi}_{BD}+\not{\nabla}_{B}\check{\chi}_{AD}-\not{\nabla}_{D}\check{\chi}_{AB}).

(9.324)

As
|\not{\Gamma}|^{2}=\not{g}^{AA^{\prime}}\not{g}^{BB^{\prime}}\not{g}_{CC^{\prime}}\not{\Gamma}_{AB}^{C}\not{\Gamma}_{A^{\prime}B^{\prime}}^{C^{\prime}}
, thus

\begin{split}\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2}|\not{\Gamma}|^{2})=&\varrho^{2}\big(\mathring{L}(|\not{\Gamma}|^{2})+\frac{2}{\varrho}|\not{\Gamma}|^{2}\big)\\
=&\varrho^{2}\big\{-4\check{\chi}^{AA^{\prime}}\not{g}^{BB^{\prime}}\not{g}_{CC^{\prime}}\not{\Gamma}_{AB}^{C}\not{\Gamma}_{A^{\prime}B^{\prime}}^{C^{\prime}}+2\not{g}^{AA^{\prime}}\not{g}^{BB^{\prime}}\check{\chi}_{CC^{\prime}}\not{\Gamma}_{AB}^{C}\not{\Gamma}_{A^{\prime}B^{\prime}}^{C^{\prime}}\\
&\qquad+2\not{g}^{AA^{\prime}}\not{g}^{BB^{\prime}}\not{g}_{CC^{\prime}}\not{g}^{CD}(\not{\nabla}_{A}\check{\chi}_{BD}+\not{\nabla}_{B}\check{\chi}_{AD}-\not{\nabla}_{D}\check{\chi}_{AB})\not{\Gamma}_{A^{\prime}B^{\prime}}^{C^{\prime}}\big\},\end{split}

which implies

|\mathring{L}(\varrho|\not{\Gamma}|)|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}(\varrho|\not{\Gamma}|)+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}.

Hence, it holds that

|\not{\Gamma}|\lesssim s^{-1}.

(9.325)

Similarly, one has

\begin{split}\mathring{L}(\not{\nabla}_{B}\not{\Gamma}_{ADC})=&[\mathring{L},\not{\nabla}_{B}]\not{\Gamma}_{ADC}+\not{\nabla}_{B}(\mathring{L}\not{\Gamma}_{ADC})\\
=&-2(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{B}\check{\chi}_{A}^{E})\not{\Gamma}_{EDC}-2(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{B}\check{\chi}_{D}^{E})\not{\Gamma}_{AEC}-2(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{B}\check{\chi}_{C}^{E})\not{\Gamma}_{ADE}+2(\not{\nabla}_{B}\check{\chi}_{DE})\not{\Gamma}_{AC}^{E}\\
&+2\check{\chi}_{DE}(\not{\nabla}_{B}\Gamma_{AC}^{E})+2\varrho^{-1}\not{\nabla}_{B}\not{\Gamma}_{ADC}+2\not{\nabla}_{B}\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}\check{\chi}_{CD}\end{split}

due to Lemma
4.8
and (
9.324
), where
\not{\Gamma}_{ADC}:=\not{g}_{DE}\not{\Gamma}_{AC}^{E}
. Thus, (
9.325
) implies that

|\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2}|\not{\nabla}\Gamma|)|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}(\varrho^{2}|\not{\nabla}\Gamma|)+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2},

which gives

|\not{\nabla}\Gamma|\lesssim s^{-2}.

(9.326)

In addition, according to the definition of Riemann curvature, one has

\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ABCD}=X_{B}\not{\Gamma}_{CDA}-X_{A}\not{\Gamma}_{BDC}-\not{\Gamma}_{CA}^{E}\not{\Gamma}_{BED}+\not{\Gamma}_{BC}^{E}\not{\Gamma}_{AED}.

(9.327)

Due to
X_{A}\not{\Gamma}_{BDC}=\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\Gamma}_{BDC}+\not{\Gamma}_{AB}^{E}\not{\Gamma}_{EDC}+\not{\Gamma}_{AD}^{E}\not{\Gamma}_{BEC}+\not{\Gamma}_{AC}^{E}\not{\Gamma}_{BDE}
, then by (
9.327
), one has

\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ABCD}=\not{\nabla}_{B}\not{\Gamma}_{ADC}-\not{\nabla}_{A}\Gamma_{BDC}+\not{\Gamma}_{BC}^{E}\not{\Gamma}_{ADE}-\not{\Gamma}_{AC}^{E}\not{\Gamma}_{BDE}.

(9.328)

Substituting the estimates (
9.325
) and (
9.326
) into (
9.328
) yields the desired result (
9.323
).
∎

Now, the following elliptic estimate will be derived.

Proposition 9.1
.

For any function
f\in C^{3}(D^{s,u})
, it holds that

\int_{S_{s,u}}|\not{\nabla}^{2}f|^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}\lesssim\int_{S_{s,u}}(\not{\triangle}f)^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}+s^{-2}\int_{S_{s,u}}|\not{d}f|^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}.

(9.329)

Proof.

For any function
f\in C^{3}(D^{s,u})
, the Ricci identity gives
\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\triangle}f=\not{\nabla}^{B}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}f-\mathcal{R}_{AB}\not{d}^{B}f
, where
\mathcal{R}_{AB}=\not{g}^{CD}\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ACBD}
is the Ricci curvature tensor. Therefore,

|\not{\nabla}^{2}f|^{2}=\not{\nabla}^{A}(\not{\nabla}^{B}f\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}f)-\not{\nabla}_{B}(\not{d}^{B}f\not{\triangle}f)+(\not{\triangle}f)^{2}-\mathcal{R}_{AB}\not{d}^{A}f\not{d}^{B}f.

(9.330)

Integrating (
9.330
) over
S_{s,u}
yields

\int_{S_{s,u}}|\not{\nabla}^{2}f|^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}=\int_{S_{s,u}}|(\not{\triangle}f)^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}-\int_{S_{s,u}}\mathcal{R}_{AB}\not{d}^{A}f\not{d}^{B}fd\nu_{\not{g}},

which implies (
9.329
) due to (
9.323
).
∎

9.1.
Estimates on derivatives of
\not{d}\textrm{tr}\chi
and
\not{\nabla}\chi

If there is at least one vector field
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{k}
, that is,
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\varrho\mathring{L}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1
and
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R_{ij}\}
, then
it follows from Proposition
8.1
and Corollary
8.1
that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
=&\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}[\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\varrho\mathring{L}},\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}]\check{\chi}+\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\varrho\mathring{L}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&s^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{1}}(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|Z^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}(\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}(\|Z^{k_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\|Z^{k_{0}}R\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+\delta s^{-2}\|Z^{k_{0}}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{d}Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\mathring{L}Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
