\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(9.331)

Thus,

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{k}\text{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{0}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(9.332)

When all vectorfields
Z
are in
\{T,R_{ij}\}
, by the expression (
5.163
), one can get the transport equation for
tr\chi
as follows

\begin{split}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}{\chi})=&-|{\check{\chi}}|^{2}-\frac{2}{\varrho}\text{tr}\chi+\frac{3}{\varrho^{2}}-F_{A\mathring{L}}\not{d}^{A}\mathring{L}\phi-G_{A\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{d}^{A}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\not{\triangle}\phi\\
&+\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\not{g}^{AB}F_{AB}\mathring{L}^{2}\phi+\frac{1}{2}\not{g}^{AB}G_{AB}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})\text{tr}\chi-(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})\text{tr}\chi+(\mathcal{FG})_{B\mathring{L}}(X_{A})\chi^{AB}\\
&+f(\phi,\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{i})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\phi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\phi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(9.333)

In addition, by (
5.130
) and (
5.128
), one can rewrite
\not{\triangle}\phi
and
\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}
as

\begin{split}&\not{\triangle}\phi=\mu^{-1}\big(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\phi+\frac{3}{2\varrho}\mathring{\underline{L}}\phi-\mathring{H}\big),\\
&\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}=\mu^{-1}\big(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{3}{2\varrho}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}-{H}_{\gamma}\big).\end{split}

(9.334)

Thus, (
9.333
) can be written as a new form which contains only the first order
or zero-th order derivatives of
\varphi_{\gamma}
on the right hand side of the following equality

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\big(\textrm{tr}\chi-E)=\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E}\big)\textrm{tr}\chi+\frac{3}{\varrho^{2}}-|\check{\chi}|^{2}+e,\end{split}

(9.335)

where

\begin{split}E=&-\not{g}^{AB}(\mathcal{FG})_{A\mathring{L}}(X_{B})+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{\underline{L}})+\frac{1}{2}\not{g}^{AB}(\mathcal{FG})_{AB}(\mathring{L}),\\
\mathcal{E}=&-\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})-(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(T),\\
e=&\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-2}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(T)+f(\phi,\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{i})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\phi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\tilde{T}\phi\\
\tilde{T}\varphi\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\phi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\end{array}\right).\\
\end{split}

Let
F^{k}=\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi-\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E
with
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R_{ij}\}
. Then by an induction
argument on (
9.335
), one has

\begin{split}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}F^{k}=&(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E})F^{k}+(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E})\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E-\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})+e^{k},\end{split}

(9.336)

where

\begin{split}e^{k}=&\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}e^{0}+\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1}{\tiny{\tiny{\tiny{\tiny}}}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]}F^{p_{2}}+\sum_{\tiny\begin{array}[]{c}p_{1}+p_{2}=k\\
p_{1}\geq 1\end{array}}\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E})\not{d}\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\textrm{tr}\chi\end{split}

(9.337)

and

\begin{split}e^{0}=&\not{d}e+(\not{d}\mathcal{E})\textrm{tr}\chi.\end{split}

(9.338)

Note that for any one-form
\xi
on
S_{s,u}
, it holds that

\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2}|\xi|^{2})=-2\varrho^{2}\check{\chi}^{AB}\xi_{A}\xi_{B}+2\varrho^{2}\not{g}^{AB}(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\xi_{A})\xi_{B}.

(9.339)

Taking
\xi=\varrho^{2}F^{k}
in (
9.339
) and using (
9.336
) show

\begin{split}&\mathring{L}\big(\varrho^{6}|F^{k}|^{2}\big)\\
=&2\varrho^{6}\big\{-\check{\chi}^{AB}F^{k}_{A}F^{k}_{B}+\mathcal{E}|F^{k}|^{2}+\not{g}^{AB}e^{k}_{A}F^{k}_{B}+(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E})\not{d}^{A}\bar{Z}^{k}E\cdot F^{k}_{A}-\not{d}^{A}\bar{Z}^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\cdot F^{k}_{A}\big\}.\end{split}

Then

\begin{split}|\mathring{L}(\varrho^{3}|F^{k}|)|\lesssim&\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\varrho^{3}|F^{k}|+\varrho^{2}|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E|+\varrho^{3}|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})|+\varrho^{3}|e^{k}|.\end{split}

(9.340)

It follows from (
8.263
) and (
9.340
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\varrho^{3}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{l}\|F^{k}(t_{0},\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l}\varrho^{3/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\Big\{\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3/2}\varrho^{3/2}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&+\tau^{1/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\tau^{3/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\tau^{3/2}\|e^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\Big\}d\tau.\end{split}

This, together with Gronwall’s inequality, yields

\begin{split}&\varrho^{3/2}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{l}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\Big\{\tau^{1/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\tau^{3/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\tau^{3/2}\|e^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\Big\}{d}\tau.\end{split}

(9.341)

Next, we come to estimate each term in the integrand in (
9.341
).

(1)

The estimate on
\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

Due to
E=-\not{g}^{AB}(\mathcal{FG})_{A\mathring{L}}(X_{B})+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{\underline{L}})+\frac{1}{2}\not{g}^{AB}(\mathcal{FG})_{AB}(\mathring{L})
, by virtue of (
5.124
), it is direct to get

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\big(\delta^{l_{-1}}\|Z^{k_{-1}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\big(\|Z^{k_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|Z^{k_{0}}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\not{d}Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|\mathring{L}Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\big)\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\mathring{\underline{L}}Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}(\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}R\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}(\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{0}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}\delta^{l_{1}}(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}},\end{split}

(9.342)

where one has used the related
L^{\infty}
estimates in Section
6
and the
L^{2}
estimates in
Proposition
8.1
, Corollary
8.1
and Lemma
8.1
,
l_{m}
is the number of
T

in
Z^{k_{m}}
(
m=-1,0,1
) and
1\leq k_{m}\leq k+1-m
.

(2)

The estimate on
\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

It follows from the structure equation (
5.167
) that

\begin{split}&\not{\nabla}_{C}\check{\chi}_{AB}=\not{\nabla}_{A}\check{\chi}_{CB}+\mathcal{I}_{CAB},\\
&\not{\nabla}^{B}\check{\chi}_{AB}=\not{d}_{A}(\textrm{tr}\chi)+\not{g}^{BC}\mathcal{I}_{CAB},\end{split}

(9.343)

where
\mathcal{I}
is a 3-form on
S_{s,u}
, whose components are

\begin{split}\mathcal{I}_{CAB}=&F_{ij}(\not{d}_{C}\phi)\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}^{i}(\not{d}_{B}x^{j})+G_{ij}^{\gamma}(\not{d}_{C}\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}^{i}(\not{d}_{B}x^{j})-F_{ij}(\not{d}_{A}\phi)\not{d}_{C}\mathring{L}^{i}(\not{d}_{B}x^{j})\\
&-G_{ij}^{\gamma}(\not{d}_{A}\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}_{C}\mathring{L}^{i}(\not{d}_{B}x^{j})-g_{ij}(\not{d}_{C}\check{L}^{i})\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}x^{j}+g_{ij}(\not{d}_{A}\check{L}^{i})\not{\nabla}_{CB}^{2}x^{j}\\
&+\varrho^{-1}(\mathcal{FG})_{BC}(X_{A})-\varrho^{-1}(\mathcal{FG})_{AB}(X_{C})+\not{\nabla}_{A}\Lambda_{CB}-\not{\nabla}_{C}\Lambda_{AB}.\end{split}

(9.344)

By commuting
{\not{\mathcal{L}}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}
with
\not{\nabla}_{C}
and
\not{\nabla}^{B}

and utilizing Lemma
4.8
, one has

\begin{split}&\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{AB}=\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{CB}+\mathcal{I}^{k}_{CAB},\\
&\not{\nabla}^{B}{\not{\mathcal{L}}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{AB}=\not{d}_{A}(\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi)+\tilde{\mathcal{I}}_{A}^{k}\end{split}

(9.345)

with

\begin{split}\mathcal{I}_{CAB}^{k}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\mathcal{I}_{CAB}+&\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{1}}\Big\{(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{C}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{A}^{D})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}_{BD}+(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{C}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{B}^{D})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}_{AD}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad-(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{C}^{D})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}_{BD}-(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{B}^{D})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}_{CD}\Big\},\\
\tilde{\mathcal{I}}_{A}^{k}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}(\not{g}^{BC}\mathcal{I}_{CAB})+&\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{1}}\Big\{\not{g}^{BC}(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{B}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{A}^{D})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}_{CD}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\not{g}^{BC}(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{B}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{C}^{D})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}_{AD}+{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}^{BC}\not{\nabla}_{B}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}\check{\chi}_{AC}\Big\},\end{split}

(9.346)

here the constant coefficients are neglected in the summation above.
Therefore,

\begin{split}|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}|^{2}=&(\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{CB}+\mathcal{I}^{k}_{CAB})(\not{\nabla}^{C}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}^{AB})\\
=&\not{\nabla}_{A}\big((\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{CB})(\not{\nabla}^{C}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}^{AB})\big)-\not{\nabla}_{C}\big((\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}^{CB})(\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{B}^{A})\big)\\
&-(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}^{CB})\not{g}^{AD}(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{ED}{\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ACB}^{E}}+\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{BE}{\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ACD}^{E}})\\
&+(\not{\nabla}_{C}{\not{\mathcal{L}}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}^{CB})(\not{\nabla}^{A}{\not{\mathcal{L}}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{AB})+\mathcal{I}^{k}_{CAB}(\not{\nabla}^{C}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}^{AB}),\end{split}

(9.347)

where the Ricci identity
\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{BD}-\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\nabla}_{A}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{BD}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{ED}{\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ACB}^{E}}+\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{BE}{\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ACD}^{E}}
with
\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ACB}^{E}=\not{g}^{EF}\not{\mathcal{R}}_{ACBF}
is used. According to the
second equation of (
9.345
) and the expressions (
9.346
), one can integrate (
9.347
) on
\Sigma_{s}^{u}
to get

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\text{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}+s^{-2}\delta^{2l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}+\delta^{2l}(\|\tilde{\mathcal{I}}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{I}}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2})\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\text{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}+s^{-2}\delta^{2l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}+\delta^{2l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{I}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-6}\delta^{2l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-6}\delta^{2l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}\\
&+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-6}\delta^{2l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}^{2}.\end{split}

(9.348)

In addition, substituting (
9.344
) into (
9.348
), due to
\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi=F^{k}+\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E
,
one can then obtain by Proposition
8.1
, Corollary
8.1
and (
9.342
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\text{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}(\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|Z^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-4}\delta^{l_{-1}}\|Z^{k_{-1}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}R\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{d}Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\text{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+s^{-1-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}}.\end{split}

(9.349)

It follows from (
9.349
) and Proposition
8.1
that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}(\not{g}^{AB}\not{g}^{CD}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}_{AC}\cdot\check{\chi}_{BD})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{l}\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{c}p_{1}+p_{2}+p_{3}+p_{4}=k\\
p_{3}\leq k-1,p_{4}\leq k-1\end{array}$}}\|\not{d}(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{1}}\not{g}^{AB}\cdot\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{2}}\not{g}^{CD}\cdot\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{3}}\check{\chi}_{AC}\cdot\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{4}}\check{\chi}_{BD})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}(\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{0}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{5/2-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}}.\end{split}

(9.350)

(3)

The estimate on
\|e^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

We first deal with the term
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]}F^{p_{2}}

in (
9.337
) when
p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1
. Due to

[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]={\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A}X_{A}
, then

\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]}F^{p_{2}}={\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{C}\not{\nabla}_{C}F^{p_{2}}+F^{p_{2}}_{C}\not{\nabla}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{C}
.
This implies

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]}F^{p_{2}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l_{2}}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k_{2}}E\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{5/2-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}}.\end{split}

(9.351)

For the term
\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E})\not{d}\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\textrm{tr}\chi

in (
9.337
) with
p_{1}+p_{2}\leq k
and
p_{1}\geq 1
, using the expression of
\mathcal{E}
and (
5.124
), one can get by Proposition
8.1
and Corollary
8.1
that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{p_{1}}(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\mathcal{E})\not{d}\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-4}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}\delta^{l_{1}}(\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\not{g}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\|\bar{Z}^{k_{1}}\check{\varrho}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\\
&+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-9/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{k_{1}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-11/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3}+s^{-5/2-\epsilon}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta s^{-5/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(9.352)

The remaining term to be estimated in (
9.337
) is
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}e^{0}
.
By (
9.338
), a direct computation yields

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}e^{0}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}}.\end{split}

(9.353)

Combining the estimates (
9.351
)-(
9.353
) with (
9.337
) leads to

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|e^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(9.354)

By inserting (
9.342
), (
9.350
) and (
9.354
) into (
9.341
), one arrives at

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(9.355)

In addition, one has from the definition of
F^{k}
that
\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi=F^{k}+\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}E
. Hence,
by (
9.355
), (
9.342
) and (
9.349
), it holds that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}{\bar{Z}}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}{\not{\mathcal{L}}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(9.356)

In summary, for any
Z\in\{\mathring{L},T,R_{ij}\}
, it follows from (
9.331
), (
9.332
) and (
9.356
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l}\|\not{\nabla}{\not{\mathcal{L}}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(9.357)

Remark 9.1
.

It should be noted here that due to
\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}=\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi

by (
4.80
), then (
9.357
) also gives the
L^{2}
estimate of
\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}
.

9.2.
Estimates on the derivatives of
\not{\triangle}\mu
and
\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu

Analogously to Section
9.1
, one can utilize the transport equation (
4.86
) for
\mu
to estimate
\not{\triangle}\mu
.
It follows from
[Z,\not{\triangle}]=-{\vphantom{{(Z)}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}-(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}{\vphantom{{(Z)}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}^{AB})\not{d}_{B}

of Lemma
4.8
with
Z\in\{T,\mathring{L}\}
that

\begin{split}&\mathring{L}\not{\triangle}\mu=[\mathring{L},\not{\triangle}]\mu+\not{\triangle}\mathring{L}\mu=\mathcal{J}_{1}+\mathcal{J}_{2}+\mathcal{J}_{3}+\mathcal{J}_{4},\end{split}

(9.358)

where
\mathcal{J}_{1}
-
\mathcal{J}_{3}
consist of all the terms whose
k
-order derivatives can not be controlled by
the estimates in Proposition
8.1
, and the remaining terms are put in
\mathcal{J}_{4}
.

•

\mathcal{J}_{1}
is comprised of all the terms containing the second order derivatives of
\mu
:

\mathcal{J}_{1}=-2\check{\chi}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\mu-2\varrho^{-1}\not{\triangle}\mu-\frac{1}{2}(\not{\triangle}\mu)\big\{(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})+2(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})\big\}.

•

\mathcal{J}_{2}
is given as follows

\mathcal{J}_{2}=-\frac{1}{2}\mu(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}+2F_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}})\underline{\mathring{L}\not{\triangle}\phi}-\frac{1}{2}\mu(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}+2G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})\underline{\mathring{L}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{2}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\underline{T\not{\triangle}\phi}+\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\underline{T\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}},

where the terms with underlines in
\mathcal{J}_{2}
are the ones including the third order derivatives of
\phi
or
\varphi
.
The following careful decomposition of
\mathcal{J}_{2}
will be used later.

It follows from (
4.86
) and (
4.92
) that

\begin{split}-\frac{1}{2}\mu F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\mathring{L}\not{\triangle}\phi=&\mathring{L}(-\frac{1}{2}\mu F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\not{\triangle}\phi)+\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}(\mu F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})\not{\triangle}\phi\\
&=\mathring{L}(-\frac{1}{2}\mu F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\not{\triangle}\phi)+f(\phi,\varphi,L^{i})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mu\mathring{L}\phi\\
\mu\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\mu\not{d}_{A}\phi\cdot\not{d}^{A}x^{j}\\
\mu\not{d}_{A}\varphi\cdot\not{d}^{A}x^{j}\\
T\phi\\
T\varphi\end{array}\right)\not{\triangle}\phi.\end{split}

(9.359)

Similar decomposition for
-\mu F_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}\mathring{L}\not{\triangle}\phi
and
-\frac{1}{2}\mu(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}+2G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})\mathring{L}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}
can be derived.

In addition, due to the equation (
5.130
), one has

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}T\not{\triangle}\phi\\
=&\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\Big(-(T\mu)\not{\triangle}\phi+\mathring{L}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi-{\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A}\not{d}_{A}\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi+\frac{3}{2\varrho^{2}}\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi+\frac{3}{2\varrho}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi-T\mathring{H}\Big)\\
=&\mathring{L}(-\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi)+\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}(\mu^{-1}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\Big((T\mu)\not{\triangle}\phi\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+{\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A}\not{d}_{A}\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi-\frac{3}{2\varrho^{2}}\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi-\frac{3}{2\varrho}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi+T\mathring{H}\Big).\end{split}

(9.360)

Analogously,
\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}
can be decomposed.

It should be emphasized that the terms like
\mathring{L}(-\frac{1}{2}\mu F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\not{\triangle}\phi)
in (
9.359
)
and
\mathring{L}(-\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi)

in (
9.360
) will be moved to the left hand side of (
9.358
).

•

\mathcal{J}_{3}
can be rewritten as

\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{3}=&-\frac{1}{2}\mu\big\{\uwave{\not{\triangle}(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})}\mathring{L}\phi+\uwave{\not{\triangle}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+\uwave{2\not{\triangle}(F_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}})}\mathring{L}\phi+\uwave{2\not{\triangle}(G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}\big\}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\uwave{\not{\triangle}(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})}T\phi+\frac{1}{2}\uwave{\not{\triangle}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})}T\varphi_{\gamma}.\end{split}

In
\mathcal{J}_{3}
, the factors with wavy line can be decomposed as follows. First, (
9.343
) implies

in\begin{split}\not{\triangle}(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})=&(\not{\triangle}F_{\alpha\beta})\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}^{\beta}+2(\not{d}_{A}F_{\alpha\beta})\not{d}^{A}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}^{\beta})+2F_{ij}(\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}^{i})\not{d}^{A}\mathring{L}^{j}\\
&+2F_{i\beta}(\not{\triangle}\check{L}^{i}+\frac{\not{\triangle}x^{i}}{\varrho})\mathring{L}^{\beta},\end{split}

(9.361)

where

\begin{split}\not{\triangle}\check{L}^{i}=&(\not{\nabla}^{B}\check{\chi}_{AB})\not{d}^{A}x^{i}+\check{\chi}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}x^{i}\\
&+\not{\nabla}^{A}\big\{-(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\tilde{T}}(X_{A})\tilde{T}^{i}-\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\tilde{T}}(X_{A})\tilde{T}^{i}+\Lambda_{AB}\not{d}^{B}x^{i}\big\}\end{split}

holds with the help of (
4.95
).
Next,
\not{\triangle}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})
,
\not{\triangle}(F_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}})
and
\not{\triangle}(G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})
have the similar expressions as that of
\not{\triangle}(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})
.
Here it is pointed out that
\|Z^{k}(\not{\triangle}(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}))\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}

can be estimated with the help of (
9.361
), (
9.357
) and Proposition
8.1
.

•

\mathcal{J}_{4}
is decomposed as

\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{4}=&-2({\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}\check{\chi}^{AB}})\not{d}_{B}\mu-\not{d}\mu\cdot\not{d}\big((\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})+2(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})\big)\\
&-\mu(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}+2F_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}})\big(\chi^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\phi+(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}\check{\chi}^{AB})\not{d}_{B}\phi\big)\\
&-\mu(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}+2G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})\big(\chi^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}+(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}\check{\chi}^{AB})\not{d}_{B}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)\\
&+\not{d}^{A}(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})\not{d}_{A}T\phi+\frac{1}{2}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\big({\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\phi+(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}^{AB})\not{d}_{B}\phi\big)\\
&+\not{d}^{A}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})\not{d}_{A}T\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\big({\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}+(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{A}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}^{AB})\not{d}_{B}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)\\
&-\mu\big\{\not{d}^{A}(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\phi+\not{d}^{A}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+2\not{d}^{A}(F_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}})\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\phi+2\not{d}^{A}(G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}\big\}.\end{split}

Finally, set

\begin{split}\tilde{E}=&-\frac{1}{2}\mu(F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}+2F_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}})\not{\triangle}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\mu(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}+2G_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma},\\
\tilde{\mathcal{E}}=&-\frac{1}{2}(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L})-(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}(\mathring{L}),\\
\tilde{F}=&\not{\triangle}\mu-\tilde{E}.\end{split}

Then by the expressions of
\mathcal{J}_{1}
-
\mathcal{J}_{4}
, (
9.358
) can be rewritten as

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\tilde{F}=&-2\check{\chi}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\mu+(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\tilde{F}-(\not{\nabla}_{B}\check{\chi}^{AB})\mathcal{Q}_{A}+\not{d}^{A}(\text{tr}\check{\chi})\mathcal{Y}_{A}+\tilde{e}\end{split}

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{A}=

\displaystyle 2\not{d}_{A}\mu+\mu(\mathcal{FG})_{A\tilde{T}}(\mathring{L})-(\mathcal{FG})_{A\mathring{L}}(T)+2\mu(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}(X_{A})+2\mu(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(X_{A}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Y}_{A}=

\displaystyle\not{d}_{A}\mu+\mu(\mathcal{FG})_{\tilde{T}\mathring{L}}(X_{A})+\mu(\mathcal{FG})_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(X_{A})

and

\begin{split}\tilde{e}=&2(\not{d}^{A}\mu)\not{d}_{A}\tilde{\mathcal{E}}+(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\tilde{E}-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}(\mu^{-1}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}})T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}(\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})T\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\big\{T\mu(\not{\triangle}\phi)+{\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A}\not{d}_{A}\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi-\frac{3}{2\varrho^{2}}\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi-\frac{3}{2\varrho}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\phi+T\mathring{H}\big\}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\big\{T\mu(\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma})+{\vphantom{{(T)}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A}\not{d}_{A}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}-\frac{3}{2\varrho^{2}}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}-\frac{3}{2\varrho}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+TH_{\gamma}\big\}\\
&+f_{1}(\phi,\varphi,L^{i},\not{d}x^{j})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mu\mathring{L}\phi\\
\mu\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\mu\not{d}\phi\\
\mu\not{d}\varphi\\
T\phi\\
T\varphi\\
\mu\chi\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{\nabla}^{2}\phi\\
\not{\nabla}^{2}\varphi\\
\varrho^{-1}\not{\triangle}x^{k}\\
\not{d}\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\mathring{L}\varphi\end{array}\right)+f_{2}(\phi,\varphi,L^{i},\not{d}x^{j})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}\phi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\not{d}\mathring{L}^{k}\\
\not{\nabla}^{2}x^{k}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}\phi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\mathring{L}\phi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}\mathring{L}^{l}\\
\check{\chi}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mu\mathring{L}\phi\\
\mu\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\mu\not{d}\phi\\
\mu\not{d}\varphi\\
T\phi\\
T\varphi\end{array}\right)\\
&+f_{3}(\phi,\varphi,L^{i},\not{d}x^{j})\not{d}\mu\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}\phi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\chi\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}\phi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\mathring{L}\phi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\end{array}\right)+f_{4}(\phi,\varphi,L^{i},\not{d}x^{j})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}T\phi\\
\not{d}T\varphi\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}\phi\\
\not{d}\varphi\\
\not{d}\mathring{L}^{k}\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(9.362)

As
F^{k}
in Sect.
9.1
, one can set
\bar{F}^{k}=\bar{Z}^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu-\bar{Z}^{k}\tilde{E}

with
\bar{Z}
being any vector field in
\{T,R_{ij}\}
. Then by an induction argument, one has that for
k\geq 1
,

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\bar{F}^{k}=&-2\check{\chi}^{AB}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\mu+(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\bar{F}^{k}-(\not{\nabla}_{B}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}^{AB})\mathcal{Q}_{A}\\
&+(\not{d}^{A}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi)\mathcal{Y}_{A}+{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A}(\not{d}_{A}\bar{F}^{k-1})+\bar{e}^{k},\end{split}

(9.363)

where

\begin{split}\bar{e}^{k}=&\underbrace{\sum_{{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{c}k_{1}+k_{2}=k-1\\
k_{1}\geq 1\end{array}$}}}(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A})(\not{d}_{A}\bar{F}^{k_{2}})}_{\textrm{vanish when}\ k=1}+\bar{Z}^{k}\tilde{e}+\sum_{{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{c}k_{1}+k_{2}=k\\
k_{1}\geq 1\end{array}$}}}\Big\{-2(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\check{\chi}^{AB})(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{2}}\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\mu)\\
&+\bar{Z}^{k_{1}}(-\frac{2}{\varrho}+\tilde{\mathcal{E}})\bar{F}^{k_{2}}-(\not{\nabla}_{B}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{2}}\check{\chi}^{AB})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{Q}_{A}+(\not{d}^{A}\bar{Z}^{k_{2}}\textrm{tr}\chi)\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}\mathcal{Y}_{A}\Big\}\\
&+\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=k-1}\bar{Z}^{k_{1}}\Big\{{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}^{BC}(\not{\nabla}_{C}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{2}}\check{\chi}_{AB})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{3}}\mathcal{Q}^{A}+\not{g}^{BC}\big(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{C}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{A}^{D}(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{2}}\check{\chi}_{BD})\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(\check{\not{\nabla}}_{C}{\vphantom{{(\bar{Z})}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{B}^{D})\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{2}}\check{\chi}_{AD}\big)\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{3}}\mathcal{Q}^{A}\Big\}\end{split}

(9.364)

when
k\geq 1
.

Due to

\begin{split}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}=G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T(\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})+2T\big(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma}\big)-2(TG_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma})T\varphi_{\gamma},\end{split}

(9.365)

then
\delta^{l}|\bar{F}^{k}(t_{0},u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}
holds with the help of (
5.124
).
Setting
F(s,u,\vartheta)=\varrho^{2}\bar{F}^{k}(s,u,\vartheta)-\varrho_{0}^{2}\bar{F}^{k}(t_{0},u,\vartheta)
in (
8.263
),
and then one can use (
9.363
) to get

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\varrho^{1/2}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{1/2}\delta^{l}\|\bar{e}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}+l}\tau^{-1/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}+l}\tau^{-1/2}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau.\end{split}

(9.366)

In the right hand side of (
9.366
), the last two integrals can be estimated by (
9.356
)
as

\begin{split}&\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}+l}\tau^{-1/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}+l}\tau^{-1/2}\|\not{\nabla}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}.\end{split}

(9.367)

In addition, it follows from (
9.329
), Lemma
4.8
and Proposition
8.1
that

\begin{split}&\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k}\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1+l-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|[\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k},\not{\nabla}^{2}]\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\delta^{1+l-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|[\bar{Z}^{k},\not{\triangle}]\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\delta^{1+l-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|\bar{Z}^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\delta^{1+l-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-5/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
\end{split}

\begin{split}\lesssim&\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+l}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|\bar{Z}^{k}\tilde{E}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\delta^{1+l-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-5/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+l}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+l}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\delta^{3/2-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}},\end{split}

(9.368)

where one has used the following fact that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{k}\tilde{E}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}-1}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{-1}}\|Z^{k_{-1}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(T)}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|\not{d}Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|TZ^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}T\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\mathring{L}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+s^{-2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}R\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{-1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}\|Z^{k_{0}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-7/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}{\vphantom{{(R)}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},\end{split}

(9.369)

which is derived by (
9.365
), (
5.124
), Proposition
8.1
and Corollary
8.1
.
By (
9.364
) and direct computations, one can estimate the
L^{2}
norm of
\bar{e}^{k}
on
\Sigma_{s}^{u}
as

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{e}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{1/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}-1}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}}\\
&+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}.\end{split}

(9.370)

Note that the term
\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}^{u})}
appeared
in (
9.370
) is derived from the term
\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\bar{Z}^{k}T\text{tr}\check{\chi}
in
\bar{e}^{k}
, which originates from
\bar{Z}^{k}(-\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}TH_{\gamma})
in
\bar{Z}^{k}\tilde{e}
.
By the identity (
5.166
),
\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\bar{Z}^{k}T\text{tr}\check{\chi}
contains
the term
\frac{1}{2}\mu^{-1}(G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}T\varphi_{\gamma})\bar{Z}^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu
which cannot be estimated
directly by Proposition
8.1
since the resulting orders of derivatives of
\mu
exceed
k+1
. In order to overcome this
difficulty, one can treat the term
\bar{F}^{k}+\bar{Z}^{k}\tilde{E}
instead of
Z^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu
.

Substituting (
9.367
)-(
9.370
) into (
9.366
) and applying Gronwall’s inequality yield

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\varrho^{1/2}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{1/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}-1}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}},\end{split}

and hence,

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{1/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)}\end{split}

with the help of (
9.369
).

For other cases containing at least one
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{k}
, for examples,
Z^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu=Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})Z^{p_{2}}\not{\triangle}\mu
with
p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1
, one can make use of the
commutators
[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]
and
[\mathring{L},\not{\triangle}]
and subsequently
utilize the transport equation (
4.86
)
to obtain the related
L^{2}
norm estimates.
Therefore, by (
9.329
) and Proposition
8.1
, we eventually obtain that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(s,u)}+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(s,u)},\end{split}

(9.371)

where
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},R_{ij},T\}
.

10.
Estimates for the error terms

After the preparations for the
L^{2}
estimates of the related quantities in Section
8
and
9
, we are ready to
handle the error terms
\delta\int_{D^{s,u}}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|
and

\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{L}\Psi|
in (
7.235
), and then get the final energy estimates for
\varphi
.

Recall
\Psi=Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}
and
\Phi=\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}
, which has been explicitly given in (
7.238
). We need to
treat each term in (
7.238
).

10.1.
Treatments on the first line of (
7.238
)

First, rewrite
Z^{k+1}
as
Z_{k+1}Z_{k}\cdots Z_{2}Z_{1}
with
Z_{i}\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R_{ij}\}
and set
\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}=\begin{cases}Z_{n}\cdots Z_{1}\varphi_{\gamma},\ &n\geq 1,\\
\varphi_{\gamma},\ &n=0.\end{cases}

(1)

By (
7.238
), one needs to treat the derivatives of
\mu\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{n}}_{,\alpha}

(0\leq n\leq k)
. To this end, one focuses first on
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}
and
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}
in (
7.242
), which do not contain the top order derivatives of
\varphi_{\gamma}
. Substituting (
4.89
)-(
4.91
) into (
7.243
) and (
7.245
) directly yields

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=&(T\mu)\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\mu(\not{d}_{A}\mu+2\mu\zeta_{A})\not{d}^{A}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})\\
&+(\not{d}_{A}\mu+2\mu\zeta_{A})\not{d}^{A}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-(T\mu)\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\mu\big({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}^{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\not{g}^{AB}\big)\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(10.372)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=&\big\{\textrm{tr}\chi T\mu+\frac{1}{4}(\mu\textrm{tr}\chi+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}-\frac{1}{2}|\not{d}\mu|^{2}-\mu\zeta_{A}(\not{d}^{A}\mu)\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{(\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\mu\\
&+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}+\mu\textrm{tr}\chi)(\not{d}_{A}\mu+2\mu\zeta_{A})-(\not{d}^{B}\mu+2\mu\zeta^{B})(2\mu\chi_{AB}+{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{AB})\big\}\not{d}^{A}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(10.373)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=&(2-\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu)\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-2\varrho(\not{d}_{A}\mu+2\mu\zeta_{A})\not{d}^{A}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\varrho\textrm{tr}\chi(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})\\
&-(2\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu)\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\mu\varrho(2\check{\chi}^{AB}-\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\not{g}^{AB})\not{\nabla}_{AB}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(10.374)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=&\textrm{tr}\chi\big\{2-\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu+\frac{1}{2}\varrho\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+2\varrho\chi_{AB}(2\mu\zeta^{B}+\not{d}^{B}\mu)\not{d}^{A}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(10.375)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R_{ij})}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=&(R_{ij}\mu)\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}\not{d}^{A}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})\qquad\quad\qquad\\
&-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}\not{d}^{A}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-(R_{ij}\mu)\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\mu({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}^{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\big(\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}\big)\not{g}^{AB})\not{\nabla}^{2}_{AB}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(10.376)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R_{ij})}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=&\big\{\textrm{tr}\chi R_{ij}\mu+\frac{1}{4}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}+\mu\textrm{tr}\chi)\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}+\frac{1}{2}\not{d}^{A}\mu{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&+\big\{2\chi_{AB}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{T}^{B}+\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}(\mu\zeta_{A}+\frac{1}{2}\not{d}_{A}\mu)+(2\mu\chi_{AB}+{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{AB}){\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{B}\quad\\
&-\textrm{tr}\chi{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{TA}-(\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\mu+\mu\text{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{2}\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}){\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}\big\}\not{d}^{A}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}.\end{split}

(10.377)

It can be checked directly that each term in
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}

contains the term
\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}

which can be treated with the help of (
5.128
) as explained after (
7.245
).
For the first line of (
7.238
), it is clear that at most
the
(k-n)^{th}
derivatives of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,i}^{n}

(i=1,3)

are taken. Thus, the
L^{2}
norms of all terms in the first line of
(
7.238
)
can be estimated by
the corresponding
L^{\infty}
estimates in Section
6

and the related
L^{2}
estimates in Proposition
8.1
, which do not need the results on the highest order derivatives
in Section
9
.
Therefore,

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}|\int_{D^{s,u}}\sum_{m=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-m}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+2-m})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,1}^{k-m}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\sum_{m=1}^{k}\|\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-m}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+2-m})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,1}^{k-m}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&\qquad\qquad\cdot\|\mathring{\underline{L}}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(10.378)

Similarly,

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}|\int_{D^{s,u}}\sum_{m=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-m}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+2-m})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,3}^{k-m}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(10.379)

The corresponding terms in
\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{L}\Psi|
can also be estimated as follows

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}|\sum_{m=1}^{k}\varrho^{2\epsilon}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-m}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+2-m})}\lambda\big)\big({\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,1}^{k-m}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,3}^{k-m}\big)\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}\Big\{\sum_{m=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-m}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+2-m})}\lambda\big)\big({\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,1}^{k-m}\\
&\qquad+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,3}^{k-m}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\delta^{2l-1}\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}|\mathring{L}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}|^{2}\\
\end{split}

\begin{split}\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3+2\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(10.380)

(2)

Next, we treat the terms
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}

(0\leq n\leq k)
. The most special case is
n=0
,
which corresponds to
m=k
in the summation of (
7.238
). In this case, the highest order derivatives
in
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}
are of order
k
. Thus there appear some terms containing the
(k+1)^{th}

order derivatives of the deformation tensors. But it can be checked by (
4.89
)-(
4.91
) that
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}
contains terms such as
\not{\triangle}\mu
and
\not{\nabla}\check{\chi}
,
which makes it difficult to apply Proposition
8.1
to estimate the
L^{2}
norm of
Z^{k}({\vphantom{D}}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0})

directly. Otherwise in the energy inequalities (
7.235
) the factors
\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+3}(s,u)
and
\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+3}(s,u)
will appear in the right
hand side, which can not be absorbed by the lower order energies on the left hand side. Thus, it is necessary to examine the expression of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k}
carefully and apply the estimates in Section
9
to deal with the higher order
derivatives of
\chi
and
\mu
.
Indeed, it follows from direct computations that

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=&\big\{\mathring{L}T\mu+\frac{1}{4}\uwave{\mathring{\underline{L}}(\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})}+\boxed{\not{\nabla}_{A}\big(\frac{1}{2}\mu\not{d}^{A}\mu}+\mu^{2}\zeta^{A}\big)\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{\frac{1}{4}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\boxed{\not{\nabla}_{A}(\not{d}^{A}\mu}+2\mu\zeta^{A})\big\}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}(2\mu^{2}\zeta_{A}+\mu\not{d}_{A}\mu)-\uline{\not{d}_{A}T\mu}\\
&+\underline{\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}(\not{d}_{A}\mu}+2\mu\zeta_{A})+\underbrace{\not{\nabla}^{B}(\mu{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\mu(\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})\not{g}_{AB})}\big\}\not{d}^{A}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(10.381)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=&\big\{\mathring{L}\big(\varrho\mathring{L}\mu-\mu\big)+\uwave{\frac{1}{2}\mathring{\underline{L}}\big(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\big)}-\varrho\boxed{\not{\nabla}_{A}\big(\not{d}^{A}\mu}+2\mu\zeta^{A}\big)\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\big(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\big)\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
-\big\{&\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\big(\varrho\not{d}_{A}\mu+2\varrho\mu\zeta_{A}\big)+\not{d}_{A}\big(\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu\big)-\varrho\underbrace{\not{\nabla}^{B}\big(2\mu\chi_{AB}-\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\not{g}_{AB}\big)}\big\}\not{d}^{A}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(10.382)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R_{ij})}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=&\big\{\mathring{L}R\mu-\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{\nabla}^{A}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}}+\frac{1}{4}\uwave{\mathring{\underline{L}}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi})}\big\}\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{\frac{1}{4}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi})\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{\nabla}^{A}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}}\big\}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{-\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}A}-\frac{1}{2}\uwave{\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}}-\boxed{\not{d}_{A}R_{ij}\mu}\\
&+\underbrace{\not{\nabla}^{B}\big(\mu{\vphantom{{\not{\pi}}}}^{(R_{ij})}{\not{\pi}}_{AB}-\frac{\mu}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}\not{g}_{AB}\big)}\big\}\not{d}^{A}\Psi_{\gamma}^{n}.\end{split}

(10.383)

Some attentions are needed to deal with the terms with underlines, wavy lines, boxes, or braces
in the above three equalities (
10.381
)-(
10.383
). In
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}
,
due to
\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\not{d}_{A}\mu=\not{d}_{A}T\mu+\frac{1}{2}\mu\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\mu
by
\mathring{\underline{L}}=\mu\mathring{L}+2T
, then the
corresponding underline part is

-\not{d}_{A}T\mu+\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\not{d}_{A}\mu=\frac{1}{2}\mu\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\mu,

(10.384)

which can be estimated by using (
4.86
).
Note that
\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}=-2\mu\not{g}^{AB}\check{\chi}_{AB}+\dots
,
\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}=2\upsilon_{ij}\not{g}^{AB}\check{\chi}_{AB}+\cdots
and
{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R_{ij})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}A}=-R_{ij}^{B}\check{\chi}_{AB}+\cdots
hold true by (
4.89
), (
4.97
) and (
4.91
),
respectively.
Then all terms with wavy lines in (
10.381
)-(
10.383
) contain the same factor
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\check{\chi}_{AB}
. Thanks to (
5.166
) and (
5.165
),
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\check{\chi}
can be replaced by
\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu+\cdots
. This leads to that in the process of estimating
\|Z^{k}({\vphantom{D}}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}
,
\|Z^{k}({\vphantom{D}}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,2}^{0})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}
and
\|Z^{k}({\vphantom{D}}^{(R_{ij})}D_{\gamma,2}^{0})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}
, the most troublesome factor related to the wavy lines in
(
10.381
)-(
10.383
) is
\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}
but which can be treated well
by (
9.371
).
In addition, the terms with boxes and braces
can be estimated by utilizing (
9.371
) and (
9.356
).

On the other hand, it is noticed that in (
10.381
)-(
10.383
), there are some terms whose factors are the derivatives of
{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(Z)}\pi

with respect to
\mathring{L}
, for example, the term
\frac{1}{4}{\mathring{L}}(\text{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}
appears in (
10.381
). In fact, these terms are not the “bad” ones since the derivatives of
\mathring{L}

for the involved deformation tensors are
actually equipped with the “good” quantities due to (
4.86
) and (
5.165
) after examining
each term in (
4.89
) and (
4.91
).

In summary, we eventually arrive at

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}|\int_{D^{s,u}}\sum_{m=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-m}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+2-m})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-m}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-6\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\end{split}

(10.385)

and

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}\sum_{m=1}^{k}|\varrho^{2\epsilon}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-m}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+2-m})}\lambda\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-m})}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-m}\cdot\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-6\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3+2\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3+2\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(10.386)

10.2.
Treatments on the other terms in (
7.238
)

It remains to treat the terms in the second and third lines in (
7.238
), which do not contain the highest order derivatives of
\textrm{tr}{\chi}
and
\not{\triangle}\mu
. Therefore, according to Proposition
8.1
and the expressions
of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,j}^{k}
in (
10.372
)-(
10.377
) and (
10.381
)-(
10.383
), we have

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{s,u}}|\sum_{j=1}^{3}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z_{k+1})}D_{\gamma,j}^{k}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}{\varphi}_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-1-\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\end{split}

(10.387)

and

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}|\sum_{j=1}^{3}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z_{k+1})}D_{\gamma,j}^{k}\cdot\varrho^{2\epsilon}\mathring{L}{\varphi}_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-4+2\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(10.388)

In addition,
\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}
is equal to
\mu\Box_{g}\varphi_{\gamma}
which is given explicitly in (
4.108
).
Then, Proposition
8.1
and (
7.239
) lead to

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{s,u}}|(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda)\cdots(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{1})}\lambda)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\end{split}

(10.389)

and

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{s,u}}\varrho^{2\epsilon}|(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\lambda)\cdots(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\lambda}}^{(Z_{1})}\lambda)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}\cdot\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3+2\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{s}\tau^{-3+2\epsilon}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(s,u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(10.390)

11.
Global existence of the solution
\phi
in
A_{2\delta}

By substituting (
10.378
)-(
10.380
) and (
10.385
)-(
10.390
) into (
7.235
), then one gets from
Gronwall’s inequality that

\delta\tilde{E}_{2,\leq 2N-4}(s,u)+\delta F_{2,\leq 2N-4}(s,u)+\tilde{E}_{1,\leq 2N-4}(s,u)+F_{1,\leq 2N-4}(s,u)\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}.

(11.391)

Hence the
L^{2}
norm of
\phi
on
\Sigma_{s}^{u}
can be estimated by (
11.391
) and the first inequality of Corollary
8.1
.
Namely, for
k\leq 2N-5
, it holds that

\delta^{l}\|Z^{k}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}}.

(11.392)

Based on (
11.391
) and (
11.392
), one can adopt the following Sobolev-type embedding formula to close the bootstrap
assumptions
(\star)
in Section
5
.

Lemma 11.1
.

For any function
f\in H^{2}(S_{s,u})
, under the assumptions
(\star)
with suitably small
\delta>0
, then

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{s,u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{s^{3/2}}\sum_{|\beta|\leq 2}\|R^{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{s,u})},

(11.393)

where
R\in\{R_{ij}:1\leq i<j\leq 4\}
.

Proof.

Its proof follows from Proposition 18.10 in
[
39
]
, details are omitted here.
∎

It follows (
11.393
), (
11.391
) and (
11.392
) that for
k\leq 2N-7
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}\varphi_{\gamma}|\lesssim\frac{\delta^{l}}{s^{3/2}}\sum_{|\beta|\leq 2}\|R^{\beta}Z^{k}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(S_{s,u})}\overset{\eqref{SSi}}{\lesssim}\frac{\delta^{1/2}}{s^{3/2}}\big(\sqrt{E_{1,\leq 2N-4}}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq 2N-4}}\big)\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2},

(11.394)

and for
k\leq 2N-8
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{1/2}s^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\sum_{|\beta|\leq 2}(\|\underline{\mathring{L}}R^{\beta}Z^{k}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})}+\|{\mathring{L}}R^{\beta}Z^{k}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s}^{u})})\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}.

(11.395)

Note that (
11.394
) and (
11.395
) are independent of
M
and hence improve the bootstrap assumptions
(\star)
. Thus we have proved the existence of the solution

\phi
to equation (
1.10
) with (
1.11
) in the domain
D^{s,4\delta}
(see Figure 6 in Section
4
).

Moreover, one can refine the estimates on
\check{\varrho}
(see (
5.121
)),
\upsilon_{ij}
(see (
4.83
)) and
g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}
,
which will be used to obtain some more precise
L^{\infty}
-estimates on
\phi
under the actions of
\Gamma\in\{L,\underline{L},\Omega_{ij}\}
.
This is one of key points to solve the global Goursat problem for
(
1.10
) in
B_{2\delta}
.

Lemma 11.2
.

In
D^{s,4\delta}
, the quantities
\check{L}^{i}
and
\check{\varrho}
admit the following estimates:

\delta^{l}|Z(g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta})|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2},\qquad\delta^{l}|Z^{k}\check{\varrho}|+\delta^{l}|Z^{k}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1},

(11.396)

where
l
is the number of
T
in
Z^{k}
, and
k\leq 2N-9
.

Proof.

Similar to (
1.2
), one can write
g_{\alpha\beta}=m_{\alpha\beta}+\tilde{g}_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\phi+O(|\phi|+|\varphi|^{2})
with
\tilde{g}_{\alpha\beta}^{\gamma}=-\tilde{g}^{\lambda\nu,\gamma}m_{\alpha\lambda}m_{\nu\beta}
. By virtue of (
4.107
), it holds that

\begin{split}g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta}&=-\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta,\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\phi\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\end{array}\right)\\
&=\mu^{-1}\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta,\gamma}\mathring{L}_{\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}(T\phi)+f(\phi,\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\phi\end{array}\right).\end{split}

As
\mathring{L}_{\gamma}=g_{\alpha\gamma}\mathring{L}^{\alpha}=\omega_{\gamma}+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega,\mathring{L}^{j})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi\\
\check{L}^{a}\\
\check{\varrho}\end{array}\right)
, the null condition (
1.16
) gives

g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta}=f(\phi,\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{j},\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\phi\\
\check{L}^{a}\varphi\\
\check{\varrho}\varphi\end{array}\right).

This, together with (
11.394
), (
11.395
) and
L^{\infty}
estimates in Section
6
, yields

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}(g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta})|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}.

In order to improve the estimate (
5.122
) for
\check{\varrho}
, we start with a part of the numerator in (
5.121
).
Due to (
4.107
), it holds that

\begin{split}&1-g_{ij}\omega^{i}\omega^{j}+2g_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\omega^{j}\\
=&\tilde{g}^{ij,\gamma}\omega_{i}\omega_{j}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)-2\tilde{g}^{0i,\gamma}\omega_{i}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)-2g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\end{array}\right)\\
=&-\mu^{-1}\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta,\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\mathring{L}_{\gamma}(T\phi)-2g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}+f(\phi,\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\phi\end{array}\right)\\
=&-2g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}+f(\phi,\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{j},\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\mathring{L}\phi\\
\not{d}\phi\\
\check{L}^{a}\varphi\\
\check{\varrho}\varphi\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(11.397)

Under the action of
\mathring{L}
,
\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}
satisfies

\begin{split}\mathring{L}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})=\big(F_{ij}\mathring{L}\phi+G_{ij}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)\varrho\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}+\frac{\varrho}{r}g_{ij}\mathring{L}(\varrho\check{L}^{i})(\mathring{L}^{j}-\check{L}^{j})+\frac{\varrho}{r}g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}(\check{L}^{j}-\omega^{j}\omega_{a}\check{L}^{a}),\end{split}

(11.398)

where
g_{ij}\mathring{L}(\varrho\check{L}^{i})\mathring{L}^{j}=-\frac{1}{2}\varrho F_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}\mathring{L}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\varrho G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+f(\phi,\varphi,\not{d}x^{i},\mathring{L}^{j})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\varrho\mathring{L}\phi\\
\varrho\not{d}\phi\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi\end{array}\right)
holds by (
4.93
).
Similar to (
5.124
),
G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}
admits the better smallness
than
\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}
due to the null condition (
1.16
). Indeed,

\begin{split}G_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}=&-(\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}\mathring{L}^{\nu}\partial_{\gamma}\varphi_{\nu}\\
=&-(\partial_{\varphi_{0}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}\big(\mathring{L}^{2}\phi-(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}\big)+\big((\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}\mathring{L}_{\gamma}\big)\tilde{T}^{i}\mathring{L}\varphi_{i}\\
&-(\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}g_{\gamma j}(\not{d}^{A}x^{j})\big(\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}\phi-(\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}\big),\end{split}

where
\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{i}=O(|\mathring{L}\phi|+|\mathring{L}\varphi|+|\not{d}\phi|+|\not{d}\varphi|)
and
(\not{d}_{A}\mathring{L}^{i})\varphi_{i}=\chi_{AB}\not{d}^{B}\phi+O(|\mathring{L}\phi|+|\mathring{L}\varphi|+|\not{d}\phi|+|\not{d}\varphi|)

due to (
4.92
) and (
4.94
) respectively, and
(\partial_{\varphi_{\gamma}}g^{\kappa\lambda})\mathring{L}_{\kappa}\mathring{L}_{\lambda}\mathring{L}_{\gamma}
satisfies (
5.125
).

At the initial time
t_{0}
,

\displaystyle\mu=\frac{1}{\sqrt{(g^{0i}\omega_{i})^{2}+g^{ij}\omega_{i}\omega_{j}}},

(11.399)

\displaystyle\tilde{L}^{i}=-g^{0i}\big(-g^{0j}\omega_{j}+\sqrt{(g^{0j}\omega_{j})^{2}+g^{ab}\omega_{a}\omega_{b}}\big)+g^{ij}\omega_{j}.

(11.400)

Note that
-1-2g^{0j}\omega_{j}+g^{ab}\omega_{a}\omega_{b}=g^{\alpha\beta}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}=\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta,\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}\phi+O(|\phi|+|\varphi|^{2})
.
Then it follows from (
4.80
) that

\begin{split}\check{L}^{i}|_{t_{0}}=&\Big\{\tilde{g}^{\alpha i,\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}\phi+\frac{1-g^{ab}\omega_{a}\omega_{b}}{2}\omega^{i}+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\end{array}\right)\Big\}\Big|_{t_{0}}\\
=&\Big\{\tilde{g}^{\alpha i,\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\partial_{\gamma}\phi-(\tilde{g}^{0j,\gamma}\omega_{j}\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\omega^{i}-\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta,\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\omega^{i}+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\end{array}\right)\Big\}\Big|_{t_{0}}.\end{split}

(11.401)

In addition, due to
\partial_{t}=-\frac{1}{2}\omega_{0}\underline{L}+\frac{1}{2}L
,
\partial_{i}=-\frac{1}{2}\omega_{i}\underline{L}+\frac{1}{2}\omega_{i}L-\frac{1}{r}\omega^{j}\Omega_{ij}

and the null condition (
1.16
), then it holds that

\begin{split}g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}|_{t_{0}}=\Big\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta,\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{\beta}\partial_{\gamma}\phi+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\phi\varphi\end{array}\right)\Big\}\Big|_{t_{0}}=f(\phi,\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\phi\varphi\\
L\phi\\
\Omega\phi\end{array}\right)\Bigg|_{t_{0}}.\end{split}

(11.402)

Furthermore, the definition of
\upsilon_{ij}
and (
11.401
) imply that

\begin{split}\upsilon_{ij}|_{t_{0}}=&\{-g_{0j}x^{i}-g_{aj}\check{L}^{a}x^{i}+g_{0i}x^{j}+g_{ai}\check{L}^{a}x^{j}-(g_{aj}-m_{aj})\omega^{a}x^{i}+(g_{ai}-m_{ai})\omega^{a}x^{j}\}|_{t_{0}}\\
=&r\Bigg\{\tilde{g}^{\alpha j,\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{i}\partial_{\gamma}\phi-\check{L}^{j}\omega_{i}-\tilde{g}^{\alpha i,\gamma}\omega_{\alpha}\omega_{j}\partial_{\gamma}\phi+\check{L}^{i}\omega_{j}+rf(\phi,\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\phi\varphi\end{array}\right)\Bigg\}\Bigg|_{t_{0}}\\
=&f(\phi,\varphi,\omega)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\phi\varphi\end{array}\right)\Bigg|_{t_{0}}.\end{split}

(11.403)

For
Z^{k}=\bar{Z}^{k}
with
\bar{Z}\in\{T=-\mu(g^{0i}+\mathring{L}^{i})\partial_{i},R_{ij}=\Omega_{ij}-\upsilon_{ij}\tilde{T}\}
,
it follows from (
11.399
) and (
11.401
)-(
11.403
) that

\delta^{l}|\bar{Z}^{k}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|_{t_{0}}\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}.

(11.404)

On the other hand, for
k\leq 2N-9
, one can get from (
11.398
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}|\mathring{L}\bar{Z}^{k}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim&\underbrace{\delta^{l}\sum_{p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1}|\big(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{p_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A}\big)\not{d}_{A}\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|}_{\text{vanish when}\ k=0}+\delta^{l}|\bar{Z}^{k}\mathring{L}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-3/2}.\end{split}

This, together with (
11.404
), yields

\delta^{l}|\bar{Z}^{k}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}.

(11.405)

Note that if there is at least one
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{k}

(k\leq 2N-8)
, i.e.,
Z^{k}=Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}

with
p_{1}+p_{2}=k-1
, then one has

(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})=\underbrace{\sum_{q_{1}+q_{2}=p_{2}-1}\varrho\bar{Z}^{q_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{A}\cdot\not{d}_{A}\bar{Z}^{q_{2}}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})}_{\text{vanish when}\ p_{2}=0}+\varrho\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}\big(\varrho^{-1}\mathring{L}(\varrho g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})-\varrho^{-1}g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}\big).

Hence by an induction argument on the number of
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{p_{1}}
, one can get from (
11.398
) and (
11.405
) that

|Z^{p_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{p_{2}}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\\
\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-l}s^{-1}.

(11.406)

Therefore, for any vector
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R_{ij}\}
, when
k\leq 2N-9
, it follows from (
11.405
) and (
11.406
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}|Z^{k}(g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j})|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}.\end{split}

(11.407)

Substituting (
11.397
) into (
5.121
) and applying (
11.407
) show that for
k\leq 2N-9
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}\check{\varrho}|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}.

∎

Lemma 11.3
.

For
k\leq 2N-10
, it holds that

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}\upsilon_{ij}|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}},

(11.408)

where
l
is the number of
T
in
Z^{k}
.

Proof.

(
4.83
) yields

\begin{split}\upsilon_{ij}=&-g_{0j}x^{i}-g_{aj}\check{L}^{a}x^{i}+g_{0i}x^{j}+g_{ai}\check{L}^{a}x^{j}-g_{aj}\frac{x^{a}x^{i}}{\varrho}+g_{ai}\frac{x^{a}x^{j}}{\varrho}\\
=&r\big(-g_{\alpha j}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{i}+g_{\alpha i}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{j}-g_{aj}\check{L}^{a}\omega^{i}+g_{ai}\check{L}^{a}\omega^{j}-\check{\varrho}g_{aj}\omega^{a}\omega^{i}+\check{\varrho}g_{ai}\omega^{a}\omega^{j}\big)\\
=&r\big(-\tilde{g}^{0j,\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}+\tilde{g}^{aj,\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}+\tilde{g}^{0i,\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}-\tilde{g}^{ai,\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho}\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}\\
&-\check{L}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}+\check{L}^{i}\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}\big)+rf(\phi,\varphi,\omega,\mathring{L}^{i},\check{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi^{2}\\
\check{L}^{a}\varphi\\
\check{\varrho}\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(11.409)

Let

A_{ij}=-\tilde{g}^{0j,\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}+\tilde{g}^{aj,\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}+\tilde{g}^{0i,\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}-\tilde{g}^{ai,\gamma}(\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho}\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}-\check{L}^{j}\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}+\check{L}^{i}\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}.

(11.410)

Then

\begin{split}TA_{ij}=&\tilde{g}^{\lambda j,\gamma}\omega_{\lambda}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}-\tilde{g}^{\lambda i,\gamma}\omega_{\lambda}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}-(T\mathring{L}^{j})\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}+(T\mathring{L}^{i})\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}\\
&+\check{\varrho}\tilde{g}^{aj,\gamma}\omega_{a}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho}-\check{\varrho}\tilde{g}^{ai,\gamma}\omega_{a}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega,\mathring{L}^{a})T(\frac{x^{b}}{\varrho}).\end{split}

(11.411)

Note that (
11.411
) contains a factor
T\mathring{L}^{i}
and
T\mathring{L}^{i}
satisfies (
4.99
).
To analyze
T\mathring{L}^{i}
, one first gets
from the definition of
G_{A\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}
in (
4.84
) and (
4.106
) that

\begin{split}&-G_{A\mathring{L}}^{\gamma}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}^{A}x^{i}=-G_{a\beta}^{\gamma}(\not{d}_{A}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}^{A}x^{i})\mathring{L}^{\beta}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\\
=&-G_{i\beta}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{\beta}(T\varphi_{\gamma})-G^{\gamma}_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}(T\varphi_{\gamma})\tilde{T}^{i}+G_{0\beta}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\tilde{T}^{i}(T\varphi_{\gamma})+f(\phi,\varphi,\mathring{L}^{i})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi\end{array}\right)T\varphi_{\gamma}\\
=&-G_{i\beta}^{\gamma}\omega^{\beta}(T\varphi_{\gamma})-G_{0\beta}^{\gamma}\omega^{\beta}\omega^{i}(T\varphi_{\gamma})+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega,\mathring{L}^{i},\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi\\
\check{L}^{a}\\
\check{\varrho}\end{array}\right)T\varphi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi\end{array}\right),\end{split}

(11.412)

where (
5.124
) is used in the last equality. Then substituting (
11.412
)
into (
4.99
) gives

T\mathring{L}^{i}=\tilde{g}^{i\lambda,\gamma}\omega_{\lambda}(T\varphi_{\gamma})-\tilde{g}^{0\lambda,\gamma}\omega_{\lambda}\omega^{i}(T\varphi_{\gamma})+f(\phi,\varphi,\omega,\mathring{L}^{i},\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho},\mu)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\phi\\
\varphi\\
\check{L}^{a}\\
\check{\varrho}\end{array}\right)T\varphi\\
T\phi\\
\mathring{L}\phi\\
\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\phi\\
\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi\\
\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\mu\end{array}\right).

(11.413)

For
k\leq 2N-10
, one can substitute (
11.413
) into (
11.411
) and use
T(\frac{x^{a}}{\varrho})=\frac{\mu\check{T}^{a}}{\varrho}+\frac{(1-\mu)x^{a}}{\varrho^{2}}
to derive that

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}TA_{ij}|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1},

where the corresponding estimates in Section
6
have been used.
Thus,

\delta^{l}|TZ^{k}A_{ij}|\leq\delta^{l}|[T,Z^{k}]A_{ij}|+\delta^{l}|Z^{k}TA_{ij}|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1},

which gives

\delta^{l}|\partial_{u}Z^{k}A_{ij}|\leq\delta^{l}|TZ^{k}A_{ij}|+\delta^{l}|\Xi^{A}\not{d}_{A}Z^{k}A_{ij}|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}.

(11.414)

Integrating (
11.414
) with respect to
u
yields

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}A_{ij}|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1},\qquad k\leq 2N-10.

(11.415)

Thanks to (
11.409
) and (
11.410
), it follows from (
11.415
) and (
11.2
) that for
k\leq 2N-10
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}\upsilon_{ij}|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}.

∎

Note that (
11.396
) implies
\check{\varrho}=\frac{r}{\varrho}-1=O(\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1})
.
Then the distance between
C_{0}
and
C_{4\delta}
on the hypersurface
\Sigma_{t}
is
4\delta+O(\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}})
and the characteristic
surface
C_{u}

(0\leq u\leq 4\delta)
is almost straight with the error
O(\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}})
away from
the corresponding light
conic surface.

On the other hand, for the standard vectors
\{L,\underline{L},\Omega_{ij}\}
defined in the end of Section
1
,
by (
4.107
) and (
4.82
),
it holds that
