\begin{split}&L=\mathring{L}-\mu^{-1}\omega^{\alpha}\mathring{L}_{\alpha}T+\omega^{\alpha}g_{\alpha j}(\not{d}^{A}x^{j})X_{A},\\
&\underline{L}=\mathring{L}+\mu^{-1}(\omega^{i}\mathring{L}_{i}-\mathring{L}_{0})T+(g_{0j}\not{d}^{A}x^{j}-\omega^{i}g_{ij}\not{d}^{A}x^{j})X_{A},\\
&\Omega_{ij}=R_{ij}+\mu^{-1}\upsilon_{ij}T.\end{split}

(11.416)

By the identity

\omega^{\alpha}\mathring{L}_{\alpha}=g_{\alpha\beta}\omega^{\alpha}\omega^{\beta}+g_{0i}\check{L}^{i}+g_{ij}\omega^{i}\check{L}^{j}+\check{\varrho}(g_{0i}\omega^{i}+g_{ij}\omega^{i}\omega^{j})

and Lemma
11.2
, when
k\leq 2N-9
, one can show that

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}(\omega^{\alpha}\mathring{L}_{\alpha})|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}s^{-1}.

(11.417)

Therefore, combining (
11.417
), (
11.408
), (
11.416
) with (
11.394
) and (
11.395
) yields that in
D^{s,u}
,

|\Gamma^{k}\varphi_{\gamma}|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}-l}s^{-3/2},\qquad|\Gamma^{k}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-l}s^{-3/2},\quad k\leq 2N-9,

(11.418)

where
\Gamma\in\{(s+r)L,\underline{L},\Omega\}
and
l
is the number of
\underline{L}
in
\Gamma^{k}
.

For any point
P\in\tilde{C}_{2\delta}=\{(t,x):t\geq t_{0},t-|x|=2\delta\}
, there is an integral line of

L
across this point, and the corresponding initial point is denoted by
P_{0}(t_{0},x_{0})
on
\Sigma_{t_{0}}
with
|x_{0}|=1
.
It then follows from (
3.58
) that
|\underline{L}^{k}L^{l}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi(P_{0})|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}
holds.
Next we derive the desired smallness and decay estimate on
\underline{L}^{k}L^{l}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi
at the point
P
.

Proposition 11.1
.

For any
(t,x)\in\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
and
3k+l+|\kappa|\leq 2N-9
, it holds that

|\underline{L}^{k}L^{l}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2-l}.

(11.419)

Moreover, when
k+l\leq 2
,

|\underline{L}^{k}L^{l}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2-l}.

(11.420)

Proof.

According to (
3.62
), it holds that

|L(r^{3/2}{\underline{L}}\phi)|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3}|r^{3/2}{\underline{L}}\phi|.

(11.421)

Integrating (
11.421
) along integral curves of
L
and applying the initial data estimate (
3.58
) on
t_{0}

yield that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|{\underline{L}}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}.

(11.422)

This, together with (
3.62
) again, shows
|L(r^{3/2}{\partial_{t}\underline{L}}\phi)|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3}|r^{3/2}{\partial_{t}\underline{L}}\phi|
.
This means that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\partial_{t}\underline{L}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}.

(11.423)

Similarly, on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\partial_{r}\underline{L}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}.

(11.424)

Thus it holds that for
(t,x)\in\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,
|\bar{\partial}\underline{L}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}

with
\bar{\partial}\in\{\partial_{t},\partial_{r}\}
. Acting
\Omega^{\kappa}
on (
3.62
) and arguing as for (
11.421
) -(
11.424
),
one can get that for
|\kappa|+3\leq 2N-9
,

|\bar{\partial}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2},\quad(t,x)\in\tilde{C}_{2\delta}.

Now we claim that when
c+2+|\kappa|\leq 2N-9
, there holds on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-3/2}

(11.425)

(both here and below, one concerns only with the numbers of
\bar{\partial}
). In fact, if one assumes that
for some positive integer
c_{0}
and
c\leq c_{0}
and
c+|\kappa|+2\leq 2N-9
, it holds that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-3/2}.

It then follows from (
3.62
) that for
c_{0}+3+|\kappa|\leq 2N-9
,

\begin{split}|L(r^{3/2}\bar{\partial}^{c_{0}+1}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}\phi)|=&|r^{3/2}\big\{\frac{3}{2}\Omega^{\kappa}[r^{-1},\bar{\partial}^{c_{0}+1}]\underline{L}\phi+\Omega^{\kappa}\bar{\partial}^{c_{0}+1}(L\underline{L}\phi+\frac{3}{2}r^{-1}\underline{L}\phi)\big\}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-(c_{0}+1)}t^{-2}.\end{split}

(11.426)

Integrating (
11.426
) along integral curves of
L
yields

|\bar{\partial}^{c_{0}+1}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-(c_{0}+1)}t^{-3/2},\quad c_{0}+3+|\kappa|\leq 2N-9,

which proves (
11.425
) by induction.

Due to (
11.425
), by checking all the terms in
L(r^{3/2}\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}\phi)
one can
obtain
|L(r^{3/2}\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}\phi)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-2}

for
c+|\kappa|+3\leq 2N-9
. Therefore, on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-3/2},\quad c+|\kappa|+3\leq 2N-9.

In addition, it follows from (
3.62
) that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}L\underline{L}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-5/2},\quad c+|\kappa|+3\leq 2N-9.

This, together with an induction argument, derives that for
c+3k+|\kappa|\leq 2N-9
and on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

\displaystyle|\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}^{k}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-3/2},

(11.427)

\displaystyle|L\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}^{k}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-5/2}.

(11.428)

Based on (
11.428
), one can assume that for some positive integer
l_{0}
and any
l
such that
1\leq l\leq l_{0}
and
l+c+3k-1+|\kappa|\leq 2N-9
,
there holds on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|L^{l}\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}^{k}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-3/2-l}.

(11.429)

For
l_{0}+c+3k+|\kappa|\leq 2N-9
and
k\geq 1
, it follows from (
11.418
), (
11.427
), (
11.429
) and (
3.62
) that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

\begin{split}&|L^{l_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}^{k}\phi|=|L^{l_{0}}\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}^{k-1}(L\underline{L}\phi)|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-5/2-l_{0}}+\sum_{c_{1}+c_{2}=c}\delta^{-c_{2}}t^{-1}|L^{l_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{c_{1}}\Omega^{\leq|\kappa|}\underline{L}^{\leq k-1}\phi|\\
&+\sum_{c_{1}+c_{2}=c}\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c_{2}}t^{-3/2}|L^{l_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{c_{1}}\Omega^{\leq|\kappa|}\underline{L}^{\leq k-1}\partial\phi|.\end{split}

Due to
\partial_{i}=\omega_{i}\partial_{r}-\frac{1}{r}\omega^{j}\Omega_{ij}
, then

\displaystyle|L^{l_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{c_{1}}\Omega^{\leq|\kappa|}\underline{L}^{\leq k-1}\partial\phi|

\displaystyle\lesssim|L^{l_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{c_{1}+1}\Omega^{\leq|\kappa|}\underline{L}^{\leq k-1}\phi|+t^{-1}|L^{l_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{\leq c_{1}}\Omega^{\leq|\kappa|+1}\underline{L}^{\leq k-1}\phi|+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c_{1}}t^{-7/2-l_{0}}.

By reducing the number of
\underline{L}
gradually, one eventually obtains that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

\begin{split}|L^{l_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{c}\Omega^{\kappa}\underline{L}^{k}\phi|\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-5/2-l_{0}}+\sum_{|\nu|+n\leq c+|\kappa|+k}\delta^{n-c}|L^{l_{0}+1}\bar{\partial}^{n}\Omega^{\nu}\phi|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-c}t^{-5/2-l_{0}}.\end{split}

This means that (
11.429
) holds for any positive
l
by an induction argument.
Then (
11.419
) follows from the second estimates in (
11.418
) together with (
11.427
) and (
11.429
) (here
c=0
is chosen).

In addition, it follow from

\begin{split}&\underline{L}(\frac{3}{r}\phi+2L\phi)\\
=&\frac{3}{r^{2}}\phi+\frac{3}{r}L\phi+\frac{2}{r^{2}}\sum_{i}\omega^{j}\omega^{k}\Omega_{ij}\Omega_{ik}\phi+2(g^{\alpha\beta,0}\phi+\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta,\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\phi+h^{\alpha\beta}(\phi,\partial\phi))\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\phi\end{split}

and (
11.418
) that

|\underline{L}L^{a}(\frac{3}{r}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi+2L\Omega^{\kappa}\phi)|_{\tilde{D}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3-a},\ 0\leq a\leq 1\ \text{and}\ a+|\kappa|\leq 2N-11,

(11.430)

where
\tilde{D}_{2\delta}=\{(t,x)|0\leq t-|x|\leq 2\delta,t+|x|\geq 2+2\delta\}
.

Integrating (
11.430
) along integral curves of
\underline{L}

yields that in
\tilde{D}_{2\delta}

|L^{a}(\frac{3}{r}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi+2L\Omega^{\kappa}\phi)|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3-a},\ 0\leq a\leq 1\ \text{and}\ a+|\kappa|\leq 2N-11,

(11.431)

which means
|L(r^{3/2}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi)|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}
.
Then we have

|\Omega^{\kappa}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}\ \text{for}\ |\kappa|\leq 2N-11

(11.432)

with the help of (
3.57
).
On the other hand, collecting (
11.432
) and (
11.431
) deduces

|L^{a}L\Omega^{\kappa}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-5/2-a},\ 0\leq a\leq 1\ \text{and}\ a+|\kappa|\leq 2N-11.

(11.433)

Utilizing (
3.62
) again, it follows from (
11.432
)-(
11.433
) and (
11.419
) that

|L(r^{3/2}\underline{L}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi)|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2},\ |\kappa|\leq 2N-15.

This implies that by (
3.56
) and when
|\kappa|\leq 2N-15
,

|\underline{L}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}.

(11.434)

Hence, by virtue of (
3.62
),

|L\underline{L}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-5/2},\ |\kappa|\leq 2N-15.

(11.435)

With the same argument as above, it holds that by the fact of
|L\underline{L}\Omega^{\kappa}(r^{3/2}\underline{L}\phi)|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}
for
|\kappa|\leq 2N-18
and (
3.56
),

|\underline{L}^{2}\Omega^{\kappa}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2},\ |\kappa|\leq 2N-18.

(11.436)

Therefore, (
11.420
) is shown.
∎

12.
Global existence of
\phi
in
B_{2\delta}
and the proof of Theorem
1.1

In this section, we will show the global existence of the solution
\phi
to (
1.10
) in
B_{2\delta}
. Set

D_{t}:=\{(\bar{t},x):\bar{t}-|x|\geq 2\delta,t_{0}\leq\bar{t}\leq t\},

see Figure 7 below. Different from the problem of 3D small value solutions inside the cone in
[
18
]
and
[
35
]
, here the solution

\phi
to (
1.10
) in
D_{t}
remains large due to the structure of the initial data on time
t_{0}
(see Theorem
3.1
).

Figure 7. The domain
D_{t}

We will adopt the energy method to get the global existence of
\phi
in
B_{2\delta}
. To this end, getting suitable rate of
time-decay of
\phi
by the Klainerman-Sobolev type
L^{\infty}-L^{2}
inequality is crucial.
However, since
D_{t}
has finite lateral boundary, then the classical Klainerman-Sobolev type
L^{\infty}-L^{2}
inequality
cannot be applied directly in
D_{t}
. Inspired by the works in
[
17
]
,
[
27
]
and
[
35
]
,
we need the following modified Klainerman-Sobolev inequalities.

Lemma 12.1
.

For any function
f(t,x)\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{1+4})
,
t_{0}\leq t\leq T
, the following inequalities hold:

|f(t,x)|\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{3}t^{-2}\delta^{(i-2)\nu}\|\bar{\Gamma}^{i}f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(r\leq t/2)},\ |x|\leq\frac{1}{4}t,

(12.437)

|f(t,x)|\lesssim|f(t,B_{t}^{x})|+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2,|\beta|\leq 1}t^{-3/2}\|\Omega^{\alpha}\partial^{\beta}f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(t/4\leq r\leq t-2\delta)},\ |x|\geq\frac{1}{4}t,

(12.438)

for
(t,x)\in D_{T}
, where
\bar{\Gamma}\in\{S,H_{i},\Omega_{ij}\}
,
\Omega\in\{\Omega_{ij}:i,j=1,2,3,4\}
,
(t,B_{t}^{x})
is the intersection point of
the boundary
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
and the ray crossing
(t,x)
which emanates from
(t,0)
,
and
\nu
is the any nonnegative constant in (
12.437
).

It should be remarked that though this Lemma and its proof are similar to those of Proposition 3.1 in
[
35
]
,
yet the refined inner estimate, (
12.437
) is new (the appearance of factor
\delta^{(i-2)\nu}
) and is crucial for
the treatment of the short pulse initial data here that is not needed for the small data case in
[
35
,
17
,
27
]

(note that in
[
35
]
, the smallness of the corresponding solution holds true in the interior domain
D(\delta)=\{(t,x):t-|x|\geq\delta\}

of the light cone due to the largeness of
N_{0}
in (
1.13
)).

Proof.

The basic approach here is similar to those for Proposition 3.1 in
[
35
]
by separating the inner estimate from the outer ones.
However, to get the refined inner estimate (
12.437
), we will use a
\delta
-dependent scaling.
Let
\chi\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}_{+})
be a nonnegative cut-off function such that
\chi(r)\equiv 1
fro
r\in[0,\frac{1}{4}]
and
\chi(r)\equiv 0
for
r\geq\frac{1}{2}
.

Define
f_{1}(t,x)=\chi(\frac{|x|}{t})f(t,x)
and
f_{2}=f-f_{1}
. Then
\text{supp}\ f_{1}\subset\{(t,x):|x|\leq\frac{t}{2}\}
and
\text{supp}\ f_{2}\subset\{(t,x):|x|\geq\frac{t}{4}\}
. One can obtain the inner estimate (
12.437
) and the outer estimate (
12.438
) separately as follows.

First, for any point
(t,x)
satisfying
|x|\leq\frac{1}{4}t
,
f_{1}(t,x)=f(t,x)
. One can rescale the variable as
x=t\delta^{\nu}y
,
and then use the Sobolev embedding theorem for
y
to get

\begin{split}|f(t,x)|&=|f_{1}(t,t\delta^{\nu}y)|\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 3}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}|\partial_{y}^{\alpha}\big(f(t,t\delta^{\nu}y)\chi(\delta^{\nu}y)\big)|^{2}dy\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 3}\Big(\int_{|\delta^{\nu}y|\leq 1/2}(t\delta^{\nu})^{2|\alpha|}|(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f)(t,t\delta^{\nu}y)|^{2}dy\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 3}\Big(\int_{|z|\leq\frac{1}{2}t}(t\delta^{\nu})^{2|\alpha|-4}|\partial_{z}^{\alpha}f(t,z)|^{2}dz\Big)^{1/2}.\end{split}

(12.439)

Note that

\partial_{i}=-\frac{1}{t-r}\big(\frac{x^{i}}{t+r}S-\frac{t}{t+r}H_{i}-\frac{x^{j}}{t+r}\Omega_{ij}\big),\quad\partial_{t}=\frac{1}{t-r}\big(\frac{t}{t+r}S-\frac{x^{i}}{t+r}H_{i}\big)

(12.440)

and
t\sim t-|z|
in the domain
\{(t,z):|z|\leq\frac{1}{2}t\}
. Then one can get

\begin{split}&|t\partial_{z}f(t,z)|\lesssim|(t-|z|)\partial_{z}f(t,z)|\lesssim|\bar{\Gamma}f(t,z)|,\\
&|t^{2}\partial_{z}^{2}f(t,z)|\lesssim\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 2}|\bar{\Gamma}^{\alpha}f(t,z)|,\\
&|t^{3}\partial_{z}^{3}f(t,z)|\lesssim\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 3}|\bar{\Gamma}^{\alpha}f(t,z)|.\end{split}

(12.441)

Therefore, substituting (
12.441
) into (
12.439
) yields (
12.437
).

Next, for
(t,x)
satisfying
|x|\geq\frac{1}{4}t
, by the Newton-Leibnitz formula and the Sobolev embedding
theorem on the circle
S_{t}^{\rho}

with radius
\rho
and center at the origin on
\Sigma_{t}
, one has

\begin{split}f^{2}(t,x)&=f^{2}(t,B_{t}^{x})-\int_{|x|}^{t-2\delta}\partial_{\rho}\big(f^{2}(t,\rho\omega)\big)d\rho\\
&\lesssim f^{2}(t,B_{t}^{x})+\int_{|x|}^{t-2\delta}\frac{1}{\rho^{3}}\sum_{|\alpha|,|\beta|\leq 2}\|\Omega^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{t}^{\rho})}\|\Omega^{{\beta}}\partial f\|_{L^{2}(S_{t}^{\rho})}d\rho\\
&\lesssim f^{2}(t,B_{t}^{x})+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2,|\beta|\leq 1}t^{-3}\|\Omega^{\alpha}\partial^{\beta}f(t,\cdot)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(t/4\leq r\leq t-2\delta)},\end{split}

which implies (
12.438
). Thus Lemma
12.1
is verified.
∎

On the hypersurface in
D_{T}
, one can have the following estimate which is similar to Lemma 2.3 for 3D case in
[
13
]
.

Lemma 12.2
.

Let
f
is the function as in Lemma
12.1
. Then for any
1\leq\bar{t}\leq t-2\delta
, it holds that

\|\frac{f(t,\cdot)}{1+t-|\cdot|}\|_{L^{2}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}\lesssim t^{3/2}\|f(t,B_{t}^{\cdot})\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}+\|\partial f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}.

(12.442)

Proof.

For any point
(t,x)
satisfying
|x|\geq\bar{t}
in
D_{T}
, one has

f(t,x)=f(t,B_{t}^{x})-\int_{r}^{t-2\delta}\nabla_{x}f(t,s\omega)\cdot\omega ds,

where
(t,B_{t}^{x})
is given in Lemma
12.1
.
Then it follows from Schwartz inequality that

|f(t,x)|^{2}\lesssim|f(t,B_{t}^{x})|^{2}+(1+t-r)^{1/2}\int_{r}^{t-2\delta}|\partial f(t,s\omega)|^{2}(1+t-s)^{1/2}ds.

(12.443)

Multiplying the factor
(1+t-r)^{-2}r^{3}
on both sides of (
12.443
) and integrating them from
\bar{t}
to
t-2\delta
yield

\begin{split}\int_{\bar{t}}^{t-2\delta}|\frac{f(t,r\omega)}{1+t-r}|^{2}r^{3}dr\lesssim t^{3}\|f(t,B_{t}^{\cdot})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}+\int_{\bar{t}}^{t-2\delta}|\partial f(t,s\omega)|^{2}s^{3}ds,\end{split}

(12.444)

which gives (
12.442
) directly by integrating (
12.444
) on
\mathbb{S}^{3}
.
∎

With these preparations and the crucial estimates in Proposition
11.1
, one can use the standard energy method to prove the global estimate of the solution
\phi
to (
1.10
) in
B_{2\delta}
. Set

E_{k,l}(t)=\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}^{2},

(12.445)

where
\tilde{\Gamma}\in\{\partial,S,H_{i}\}
and
k+l\leq 6
. By
the estimates (
3.57
) on
\Sigma_{t_{0}}
, one can make the following bootstrap assumptions:

For
t\geq t_{0}
and
0<\varepsilon_{0}\leq\frac{7}{144}
, there exists a uniform constant
M_{0}>0
such that

\begin{split}&E_{0,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{1,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{44/9-10\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{2,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{8/3-2\varepsilon_{0}},\\
&E_{3,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{4,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{-1-2\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad\quad E_{5,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{-3-2\varepsilon_{0}},\\
&E_{6,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{-5-2\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(12.446)

Lemma
12.1
,
12.2
, and the assumptions (
12.446
) imply
the following
L^{\infty}
estimates.

Proposition 12.1
.

Under the assumptions (
12.446
) with suitably small
\delta>0
, it holds that

\begin{split}&|\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{(4-5\varepsilon_{0})/3}t^{-3/2},\ \ \quad|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq 2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2},\\
&|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-5/9-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2},\quad|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{3}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}.\end{split}

(12.447)

Proof.

First, for
|x|\leq\frac{t}{4}
, one gets from (
12.437
) that

\begin{split}|\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi|\lesssim t^{-2}\Big\{&\delta^{-2\nu}\|\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}+\delta^{-\nu}\|\bar{\Gamma}\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}\\
&+\|\bar{\Gamma}^{2}\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}+\delta^{\nu}\|\bar{\Gamma}^{3}\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}\big\}.\end{split}

(12.448)

Choosing
\nu=\frac{5}{6}-\frac{2}{3}\varepsilon_{0}
in (
12.448
) and utilizing the assumptions (
12.446
) yield

|\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{(4-5\varepsilon_{0})/3}t^{-2}.

(12.449)

Next, for
\frac{t}{4}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta
, choosing
f(t,x)
as
\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi(t,x)
in (
12.438
), one can get,

\begin{split}|\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim&|\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi(t,B_{t}^{x})|+t^{-3/2}\|\Omega^{\leq 2}\partial^{\leq 1}\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\frac{t}{4}\leq r\leq t)}\\
\lesssim&M_{0}\delta^{44/9-10\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}\end{split}

(12.450)

with the help of (
11.420
).
Combining (
12.449
) and (
12.450
) yields

\displaystyle|\partial\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{(4-5\varepsilon_{0})/3}t^{-3/2}.

Finally, the other cases can be treated similarly as in (
12.448
) by choosing different
\nu
such as
\nu=\frac{11}{12}

for
\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq 2}\phi
,
\nu=\frac{17}{18}
for
\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi
, and
\nu=1
for
\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{3}\phi
. Due to the direct but tedious computations, the resulting details are omitted.
∎

Remark 12.1
.

It is pointed out that the assumption of
\varepsilon_{0}\leq\frac{7}{144}
is used to derive
|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq 2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}
in (
12.447
) when
\nu=\frac{11}{12}
is chosen according to the analogous inequality (
12.448
).

Corollary 12.1
.

Under the same conditions in Proposition
12.1
, it holds that

\begin{split}&|\partial^{2}\Omega^{\leq 2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r)^{-1},\\
&|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-5/9-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r)^{-1},\\
&|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r)^{-1}\end{split}

(12.451)

and

\begin{split}&|\Omega^{\leq 3}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{(4-5\varepsilon_{0})/3}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\\
&|\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq 2}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\\
&|\tilde{\Gamma}^{2}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-5/9-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\\
&|\tilde{\Gamma}^{3}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r).\end{split}

(12.452)

Proof.

(
12.451
) follow from (
12.440
) and (
12.447
) directly. (
12.452
) can be shown by using

f(t,x)=f(t,B_{t}^{x})-\int_{r}^{t-2\delta}\nabla_{x}f(t,s\omega)\cdot\omega ds

and (
12.447
).
∎

We now carry out the energy estimates on
\phi
in
B_{2\delta}
.
Direct computations show

\begin{split}(\partial_{t}v)g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}v=&\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\big((g^{00}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-g^{ij}\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v)\big)+\partial_{i}\big((g^{0i}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}+g^{ij}\partial_{t}v\partial_{j}v)\big)\\
&+\big(-(\partial_{i}g^{0i})(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\partial_{t}v\partial_{j}v+\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}g^{ij})\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v\big),\end{split}

(12.453)

where

\begin{split}-(\partial_{i}g^{0i})(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\partial_{t}v\partial_{j}v+\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{t}g^{ij})\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v=O\big((|\partial\phi|+|\partial^{2}\phi|)|\partial v|^{2}\big).\end{split}

(12.454)

In addition, integrating (
12.453
) over
D_{t}
and using integration by parts give rise to a term on the lateral boundary
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
as

\begin{split}&-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{4}(g^{00}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}-g^{ij}\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v)+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}(g^{0i}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}+g^{ij}\partial_{t}v\partial_{j}v)\omega_{i}\\
=&\frac{\sqrt{2}}{4}(Lv)^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{4r^{2}}\sum_{i}(\omega^{j}\Omega_{ij}v)^{2}+O\big((|\phi|+|\partial\phi|)|\partial v|^{2}\big).\end{split}

(12.455)

With the help of (
11.420
), it can be checked that the term in (
12.455
) can be controlled on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
by

(Lv)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}(\frac{\omega^{j}}{r}\Omega_{ij}v)^{2}+\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(\partial v)^{2},

(12.456)

where the unimportant constant coefficients in (
12.456
) are neglected.

Due to (
12.454
) and (
12.456
), integrating (
12.453
) on
D_{t}
and
utilizing Proposition
12.1
and Corollary
12.1
,
one then gets from Gronwall’s inequality that for small
\delta>0
,

\begin{split}&\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t}}\big((\partial_{t}v)^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\big)\\
\lesssim&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D_{t}}\big((\partial_{t}v)^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\big)+\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial_{t}vg^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}v|\\
&+\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}\big\{(Lv)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}(\frac{\omega^{j}}{r}\Omega_{ij}v)^{2}+\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}tau^{-3/2}|\partial v|^{2}\big\}.\end{split}

(12.457)

To close the bootstrap assumptions (
12.446
), one can set
v=\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi

(k+l\leq 6)
in (
12.457
).
Note that (
11.419
) and (
11.420
) implies that
(L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}(\frac{\omega^{j}}{r}\Omega_{ij}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|^{2}\lesssim\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-9/2}
for
k\leq 1
and
(L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}(\frac{\omega^{j}}{r}\Omega_{ij}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|^{2}\lesssim\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-9/2}
for
2\leq k\leq 6
hold
on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
. Therefore,

\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}\big\{(L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{4}(\frac{\omega^{j}}{r}\Omega_{ij}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3/2}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|^{2}\big\}\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} &\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}},\ k\leq 1,\\
&\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}},\ 2\leq k\leq 6.\end{aligned}\right.

In addition, on the initial hypersurface
\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D_{t}
,
it holds that
|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{3-k-\varepsilon_{0}}

for
0\leq k\leq 6-l
by (
3.57
). Hence, (
12.457
) gives that

\displaystyle E_{0,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}}+\iint_{D_{t}}|(\partial_{t}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\Omega^{l}\phi)|,\quad l\leq 6,

(12.458)

\displaystyle E_{k,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{7-2k-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\iint_{D_{t}}|(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)|,\quad 1\leq k\leq 6-l.

(12.459)

It remains to estimate
\iint_{D_{t}}|(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)|

in the right hand sides of (
12.458
)-(
12.459
) and further obtain the global existence of
\phi
in
B_{2\delta}
.

Theorem 12.1
.

When
\delta>0
is small, there exists a smooth solution
\phi
to (
1.10
) in
B_{2\delta}
.

Proof.

Acting
\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}
on (
1.10
) and commuting it with
g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}
yield

\begin{split}|g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k,l_{1}+l_{2}\leq l\\
k_{2}+l_{2}<k+l\end{array}$}}|\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|.\end{split}

The right hand side can be estimated separately as follows.

(1)

k=0
and
l\leq 6
.

(a)

When
l_{1}\leq l_{2}
, then
l_{1}\leq 3
holds. It follows from Proposition
12.1
and Corollary
12.1
that

|\Omega^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\lesssim|\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\phi|+|\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\partial\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{(4-5\varepsilon_{0})/3}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r).

Thus, since
l_{2}\leq l-1
, one has by (
12.446
) that

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{\Omega}^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\cdot|\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\\
\lesssim&\int_{t_{0}}^{t}M_{0}\delta^{(4-5\varepsilon_{0})/3}\tau^{-3/2}\|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}\|(1+\tau-r)\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}d\tau\\
\lesssim&M_{0}\delta^{(4-5\varepsilon_{0})/3}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}\sqrt{E_{0,\leq 6}(\tau)}\big(\sqrt{E_{0,\leq 6}(\tau)}+\sqrt{E_{1,\leq 5}(\tau)}\big)d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(12.460)

(b)

When
l_{1}>l_{2}
, then
l_{2}\leq 2
holds. It follows from Corollary
12.1
that

|\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r)^{-1}.

This, together with (
12.446
) and Lemma
12.2
, yields

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{\Omega}^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\cdot|\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\\
\lesssim&M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}\iint_{D_{t}}\tau^{-3/2}(1+\tau-r)^{-1}|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi|\big(|\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\phi|+|\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\partial\phi|\big)\\
\lesssim&M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}\|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi(\tau,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}\Big(\|\frac{\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\phi}{1+\tau-|\cdot|}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+\|\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\partial\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}\Big)d\tau\\
\lesssim&M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}\|\partial\Omega^{l}\phi(\tau,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}(\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\|\partial\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})})d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(12.461)

Inserting (
12.460
) and (
12.461
) into (
12.458
) leads to

E_{0,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}},\quad l\leq 6.

(12.462)

(2)

k=1
and
l\leq 5
.

(a)

When
k_{1}+l_{1}\leq k_{2}+l_{2}
, then
k_{1}+l_{1}\leq 3
holds. Due to
k_{1}\leq 1
, it follows from
Proposition
12.1
and Corollary
12.1
that

|\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\lesssim|\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\phi|+|\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\partial\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r).

Then, since
k_{2}+l_{2}\leq 5
and
\delta>0
is small, one has

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\\
\lesssim&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi|\big\{|\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\cdot|\partial^{2}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+|\Omega^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{\leq 1}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\big\}\\
\lesssim&M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{\leq 1}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}d\tau\\
&+M_{0}\delta^{(4-5\varepsilon_{0})/3}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{\leq 2}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap D_{t})}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{9/44-10\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(12.463)

(b)

When
k_{1}+l_{1}>k_{2}+l_{2}
, then
k_{2}+l_{2}\leq 2
holds. As
k_{2}\leq 1
, then Corollary
12.1
implies

|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{-5/9-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r)^{-1}.

This, together with (
12.446
), yields

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}(g^{\alpha\beta}-m^{\alpha\beta})|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\\
\lesssim&M_{0}\delta^{5/12-\varepsilon_{0}}\iint_{D_{t}}\tau^{-3/2}(1+\tau-r)^{-1}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi|\big(|\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\phi|+|\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\partial\phi|\big)\\
&+M_{0}\delta^{-5/9-\varepsilon_{0}}\iint_{D_{t}}\tau^{-3/2}(1+\tau-r)^{-1}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi|\big(|\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\phi|+|\Omega^{\leq l_{1}}\partial\phi|\big)\\
\lesssim&\delta^{9/44-10\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(12.464)

Similarly to Case (1), substituting (
12.463
) and (
12.464
) into (
12.459
) gives

E_{1,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{9/44-10\varepsilon_{0}},\quad l\leq 5.

(12.465)

(3)

k\geq 2
and
k+l\leq 6
.

When
k+l\leq 6

(k\geq 2)
, as in the case (1) and (2), one can make use of Proposition
12.1
and Corollary
12.1
to estimate the
related integrals in (
12.458
)-(
12.459
) for
k_{1}+l_{1}\leq k_{2}+l_{2}
(
k_{1}+l_{1}\leq 3
and
k_{2}+l_{2}\leq 5
), meanwhile utilize Corollary
12.1

to deal with the remaining terms (
k_{2}+l_{2}\leq 2
).

Consequently, one has obtained the following desired estimates:

\begin{split}&E_{0,l}(t)\leq\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{1,l}(t)\leq\delta^{44/9-10\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{2,l}(t)\leq\delta^{8/3-2\varepsilon_{0}},\\
&E_{3,l}(t)\leq\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad E_{4,l}(t)\leq\delta^{-1-2\varepsilon_{0}},\qquad\quad E_{5,l}(t)\leq\delta^{-3-2\varepsilon_{0}},\\
&E_{6,l}(t)\leq\delta^{-5-2\varepsilon_{0}}.\end{split}

(12.466)

Since the constants in (
12.466
) are all independent of
M_{0}
, the bootstrap
assumptions (
12.446
) can be proved as long as (
12.446
) holds for the time
t=t_{0}
. By combining the
local existence of the solution
\phi

to the problem (
1.10
) by a continuous induction argument, the global existence
of
\phi
in
B_{2\delta}
is established.
∎

We now prove Theorem
1.1
.

Proof.

By Theorem
3.1
, one has obtained the local existence of smooth solution
\phi
to equation (
1.10
) with (
1.11
).
On the other hand, the global existence of
\phi
in
A_{2\delta}

and
B_{2\delta}
are established in Section
11
and Section
12
respectively.
Then it follows from the uniqueness of smooth solutions to (
1.10
) with (
1.11
)
that the proof of
\phi\in C^{\infty}([1,+\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{4})
is finished.
In addition,
|\partial\phi|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}
comes from (
3.59
), (
11.395
) and the first inequality in (
12.447
);

|\phi|\lesssim\delta^{\frac{7}{6}-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}
is derived from (
3.59
), (
11.395
) and the first inequality in (
12.452
). Thus Theorem
1.1
is proved.
∎
